<script id="ethvelle-v128-model-parts-held-items-runtime">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const safe=v=>String(v==null?'':v).replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
  const currentAnimClip=()=>{
    if(typeof selectedClip!=='string'||!(selectedClip==='main'||/^seg\d+$/.test(selectedClip)))return null;
    return typeof getClipByRef==='function'?getClipByRef(selectedClip):null;
  };
  const currentChar=()=>typeof activeCharacter==='function'?activeCharacter():null;
  const charParts=c=>c&&c.sheet&&c.sheet.parts?c.sheet.parts:{};
  const actionDefaultsV128=()=>({expression:'none',mouth:'preset',blink:'auto',eyes:'auto',wings:'natural',leftHand:'none',rightHand:'none'});
  const normalizeActionsV128=input=>{
    const a={...actionDefaultsV128(),...(input&&typeof input==='object'?input:{})};
    if(!['none','sad','angry','shock','laugh'].includes(a.expression))a.expression='none';
    if(!['preset','talk','closed','hold'].includes(a.mouth))a.mouth='preset';
    if(!['auto','ambient','off'].includes(a.blink))a.blink='auto';
    if(!['auto','open','closed'].includes(a.eyes))a.eyes='auto';
    if(!['natural','fly'].includes(a.wings))a.wings='natural';
    ['leftHand','rightHand'].forEach(k=>{if(!['none','point','thumb','palm','fist','hold'].includes(a[k]))a[k]='none';});
    return a;
  };
  function choiceTile(key,label,src,active,handler,icon,compose){
    return `<button type="button" class="evpa-choice ${active?'active':''}" aria-pressed="${active?'true':'false'}"${compose?` data-compose="${safe(compose)}"`:''} onclick="${handler}"><span class="evpa-choice-preview">${src?`<img src="${safe(src)}" alt="">`:`<span class="evpa-choice-icon">${icon||'◌'}</span>`}</span><span class="evpa-choice-label">${safe(label)}</span></button>`;
  }
  /* v330: the face/hand panel reads the CURRENT humanoid (14 + 19 part) and bird (7 + 7 part) sheet structure. */
  const typeOfChar=c=>String(c&&(c.modelType||(c.sheet&&c.sheet.modelType))||'humanoid');
  const hasBirdKit=p=>!!(p&&p.bird_eye&&p.bird_brow&&p.beak_closed&&p.beak_open);
  const hasHumanoidKit=p=>!!(p&&p.mouth_inner&&p.eye_L&&p.eye_R&&p.brow_L&&p.brow_R);
  function faceChoiceGrid(c,a){
    const parts=charParts(c),type=typeOfChar(c),birdKit=type==='bird'&&hasBirdKit(parts),humKit=type==='humanoid'&&hasHumanoidKit(parts);
    const compose=e=>(birdKit||humKit)?'emo:'+e+'|mouth:'+(birdKit&&['angry','shock','laugh'].includes(e)?'open':'closed')+'|eyes:open':'';
    const defs=[['none','Original',parts.head,'🙂'],['sad','Sad',parts.head_sad,'🙁'],['angry','Angry',parts.head_angry,'😠'],['shock','Surprised',parts.head_shock,'😮'],['laugh','Laughing',parts.head_laugh,'😄']];
    /* Birds have no swap-heads: their expressions are drawn from the brow + beak + eye parts, so all four are offered. */
    return defs.filter(([v,l,part])=>v==='none'||birdKit||!!part).map(([v,l,part,ico])=>choiceTile('face-'+v,l,part&&part.src,a.expression===v,`updateClipFaceAction('expression','${v}')`,ico,compose(v))).join('');
  }
  function eyeChoiceGrid(c,a){
    const p=charParts(c),type=typeOfChar(c);
    if(type==='bird'&&p.bird_eye){
      return [
        choiceTile('eyes-auto','Auto',p.bird_eye.src,a.eyes==='auto',"updateClipFaceAction('eyes','auto')",'◉'),
        choiceTile('eyes-open','Eyes open',p.bird_eye.src,a.eyes==='open',"updateClipFaceAction('eyes','open')",'◉'),
        choiceTile('eyes-closed','Eyes closed',p.bird_eye_closed&&p.bird_eye_closed.src,a.eyes==='closed',"updateClipFaceAction('eyes','closed')",' - ')
      ].join('');
    }
    if((type==='animal'||type==='humanoid')&&p.eye_L&&p.eye_R){
      const closedPreview=p.eye_closed_L&&p.eye_closed_R&&p.eye_closed_L.src;
      const items=[
        choiceTile('eyes-auto','Auto',p.eye_L.src,a.eyes==='auto',"updateClipFaceAction('eyes','auto')",'◉'),
        choiceTile('eyes-open','Eyes open',p.eye_L.src,a.eyes==='open',"updateClipFaceAction('eyes','open')",'◉'),
        ...(closedPreview?[choiceTile('eyes-closed','Eyes closed',closedPreview,a.eyes==='closed',"updateClipFaceAction('eyes','closed')",' - ')]:[])
      ];
      return items.join('');
    }
    const items=[choiceTile('eyes-auto','Auto',p.head&&p.head.src,a.eyes==='auto',"updateClipFaceAction('eyes','auto')",'◉')];
    if(p.head_open)items.push(choiceTile('eyes-open','Eyes open',p.head_open.src,a.eyes==='open',"updateClipFaceAction('eyes','open')",'◉'));
    if(p.head_blink)items.push(choiceTile('eyes-closed','Eyes closed',p.head_blink.src,a.eyes==='closed',"updateClipFaceAction('eyes','closed')",' - '));
    return items.join('');
  }
  function blinkChoiceGrid(a){
    return [
      choiceTile('blink-auto','Clip blinking',null,a.blink==='auto',"updateClipFaceAction('blink','auto')",'◉'),
      choiceTile('blink-ambient','Ambient',null,a.blink==='ambient',"updateClipFaceAction('blink','ambient')",'◌'),
      choiceTile('blink-off','Off',null,a.blink==='off',"updateClipFaceAction('blink','off')",' - ')
    ].join('');
  }
  function mouthChoiceGrid(c,a){
    const p=charParts(c),type=typeOfChar(c);
    if(type==='animal'&&p.mouth_inner){
      return [
        choiceTile('mouth-preset','Expression mouth',p.mouth_inner.src,a.mouth==='preset',"updateClipFaceAction('mouth','preset')",'◡'),
        choiceTile('mouth-talk','Talk loop',p.mouth_inner.src,a.mouth==='talk',"updateClipFaceAction('mouth','talk')",'◉'),
        choiceTile('mouth-closed','Hide inner mouth',null,a.mouth==='closed',"updateClipFaceAction('mouth','closed')",' - ')
      ].join('');
    }
    if(type==='humanoid'&&hasHumanoidKit(p)){
      /* Current humanoid kit: the mouth is the inner-mouth layer shown through the open expression heads (talk = laughing head). */
      return [
        choiceTile('mouth-preset','Animation',null,a.mouth==='preset',"updateClipFaceAction('mouth','preset')",'◡','emo:none|mouth:closed|eyes:open'),
        ...(p.head_laugh?[choiceTile('mouth-talk','Talk loop',p.mouth_inner.src,a.mouth==='talk',"updateClipFaceAction('mouth','talk')",'◉','emo:laugh|mouth:open|eyes:open')]:[]),
        choiceTile('mouth-closed','Closed',null,a.mouth==='closed',"updateClipFaceAction('mouth','closed')",' - ','emo:none|mouth:closed|eyes:open')
      ].join('');
    }
    if(type==='bird'&&hasBirdKit(p)){
      /* Current bird kit: closed beak and open beak are separate nested parts. */
      return [
        choiceTile('mouth-preset','Animation',p.beak_closed.src,a.mouth==='preset',"updateClipFaceAction('mouth','preset')",'◡','emo:none|mouth:closed|eyes:open'),
        choiceTile('mouth-talk','Talk loop',p.beak_open.src,a.mouth==='talk',"updateClipFaceAction('mouth','talk')",'◉','emo:none|mouth:open|eyes:open'),
        choiceTile('mouth-closed','Closed',p.beak_closed.src,a.mouth==='closed',"updateClipFaceAction('mouth','closed')",' - ','emo:none|mouth:closed|eyes:open')
      ].join('');
    }
    return [
      choiceTile('mouth-preset','Animation',p.mouth_closed&&p.mouth_closed.src,a.mouth==='preset',"updateClipFaceAction('mouth','preset')",'◡'),
      ...(p.mouth_open?[choiceTile('mouth-talk','Talk loop',p.mouth_open.src,a.mouth==='talk',"updateClipFaceAction('mouth','talk')",'◉')]:[]),
      choiceTile('mouth-closed','Closed',p.mouth_closed&&p.mouth_closed.src,a.mouth==='closed',"updateClipFaceAction('mouth','closed')",' - ')
    ].join('');
  }
  function wingChoiceGrid(c,a){
    const p=charParts(c),near=p.armL_upper||p.armR_upper,fly=p.wingL_fly||p.wingR_fly;
    return [
      choiceTile('wings-natural','Default wings',near&&near.src,(a.wings||'natural')==='natural',"updateClipFaceAction('wings','natural')",'🪽'),
      ...(fly?[choiceTile('wings-fly','Flying wings',fly.src,a.wings==='fly',"updateClipFaceAction('wings','fly')",'🕊')]:[])
    ].join('');
  }
  function mouthItemSection(c){
    return ''; /* v298: "Hold an item with the mouth" section removed */
    const type=String(c&&(c.modelType||(c.sheet&&c.sheet.modelType))||'humanoid');if(type!=='animal'&&type!=='bird')return '';
    const item=c.mouthProp,scale=Math.round((Number(item&&item.scale)||1)*100),rotation=Math.round(Number(item&&item.rotation)||0),x=Math.round(Number(item&&item.offsetX)||0),y=Math.round(Number(item&&item.offsetY)||0);
    return `<section class="evpa-section evca-mouth-item-section"><p class="evpa-section-title">Hold an item with the ${type==='bird'?'beak':'mouth'}</p><input id="evcaMouthItemFile" type="file" accept="image/png,image/webp,image/jpeg,image/*" hidden onchange="setCharacterMouthItemFile(this)"><button type="button" class="evpa-prop-upload" onclick="document.getElementById('evcaMouthItemFile').click()">＋ ${item?'Replace mouth item':'Add mouth item'}</button>${item?`<div class="evca-mouth-item-preview"><img src="${safe(item.src)}" alt="Mouth-held item"><button type="button" onclick="removeCharacterMouthItem()">Remove</button></div>`:''}<div class="evca-mouth-item-controls" ${item?'':'style="opacity:.45;pointer-events:none"'}><label>Size <output id="evcaMouthScaleVal">${scale}%</output><input type="range" min="20" max="220" value="${scale}" oninput="setCharacterMouthItemValue('scale',Number(this.value)/100)"></label><label>Rotate <output id="evcaMouthRotationVal">${rotation}°</output><input type="range" min="-180" max="180" value="${rotation}" oninput="setCharacterMouthItemValue('rotation',Number(this.value))"></label><label>Move X <output id="evcaMouthXVal">${x}</output><input type="range" min="-100" max="100" value="${x}" oninput="setCharacterMouthItemValue('offsetX',Number(this.value))"></label><label>Move Y <output id="evcaMouthYVal">${y}</output><input type="range" min="-100" max="100" value="${y}" oninput="setCharacterMouthItemValue('offsetY',Number(this.value))"></label></div></section>`;
  }
  window.setCharacterMouthItemFile=function(input){
    const file=input&&input.files&&input.files[0];if(input)input.value='';if(!file)return;const c=currentChar();if(!c)return;
    const type=String(c.modelType||(c.sheet&&c.sheet.modelType)||'humanoid');if(type!=='animal'&&type!=='bird')return;
    const reader=new FileReader();reader.onerror=()=>{if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Could not read the mouth item image',{icon:toastIcons.info});};
    reader.onload=()=>{const img=new Image();img.onerror=()=>{if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Could not decode the mouth item image',{icon:toastIcons.info});};img.onload=()=>{
      const max=640,ratio=Math.min(1,max/Math.max(img.naturalWidth,img.naturalHeight)),w=Math.max(1,Math.round(img.naturalWidth*ratio)),h=Math.max(1,Math.round(img.naturalHeight*ratio)),cv=document.createElement('canvas');cv.width=w;cv.height=h;const ctx=cv.getContext('2d');if(!ctx)return;ctx.clearRect(0,0,w,h);ctx.drawImage(img,0,0,w,h);
      c.mouthProp={src:cv.toDataURL('image/png'),name:file.name||'Mouth item',scale:1,rotation:0,offsetX:0,offsetY:0,flipX:false,flipY:false};
      const clip=currentAnimClip();if(clip){const a=normalizeActionsV128(clip.faceActions);clip.faceActions={...a,mouth:'hold'};}
      if(!c.expr)c.expr=typeof sheetExprFresh==='function'?sheetExprFresh():{};c.expr.mouth='hold';
      if(typeof drawStick==='function')drawStick();if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState();window.openClipCharacterActions();
      if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Item attached to the '+(type==='bird'?'beak':'mouth'),{icon:toastIcons.check,duration:1500});
    };img.src=String(reader.result||'');};reader.readAsDataURL(file);
  };
  window.setCharacterMouthItemValue=function(field,value){const c=currentChar(),item=c&&c.mouthProp;if(!item||!['scale','rotation','offsetX','offsetY'].includes(field)||!Number.isFinite(Number(value)))return;item[field]=Number(value);const id={scale:'evcaMouthScaleVal',rotation:'evcaMouthRotationVal',offsetX:'evcaMouthXVal',offsetY:'evcaMouthYVal'}[field],out=$(id);if(out)out.textContent=field==='scale'?Math.round(item.scale*100)+'%':field==='rotation'?Math.round(item.rotation)+'°':String(Math.round(item[field]));if(typeof drawStick==='function')drawStick();if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState();};
  window.removeCharacterMouthItem=function(){const c=currentChar();if(!c)return;c.mouthProp=null;const clip=currentAnimClip();if(clip){const a=normalizeActionsV128(clip.faceActions);clip.faceActions={...a,mouth:'closed'};}if(typeof drawStick==='function')drawStick();if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState();window.openClipCharacterActions();};
  function handChoiceGrid(c,a,side){
    const p=charParts(c),field=side==='L'?'leftHand':'rightHand',base='hand'+side;
    const items=[['none','Natural',p[base],'✋'],['fist','Fist',p[base+'_fist'],'✊'],['point','Point',p[base+'_point'],'☝'],['thumb','Thumbs up',p[base+'_thumb'],'👍'],['palm','Open palm',p[base+'_palm'],'🖐'],['hold','Holding grip',p[base+'_hold'],'🤏']];
    return items.filter(([v,l,part])=>v==='none'||!!part).map(([v,l,part,ico])=>choiceTile('hand'+side+'-'+v,l,part&&part.src,a[field]===v,`updateClipFaceAction('${field}','${v}')`,ico)).join('');
  }
  function ensureFaceBackdrop(){
    let el=$('ethvelleClipFaceActionsBackdrop');
    if(!el){const host=$('screen-editor')||document.body;el=document.createElement('div');el.id='ethvelleClipFaceActionsBackdrop';el.className='modal-backdrop evca-char-actions-backdrop';host.appendChild(el);el.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target===el)closeClipCharacterActions();});}
    return el;
  }
  window.openClipCharacterActions=function(){
    const clip=currentAnimClip(),c=currentChar();
    if(!clip||!c){if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Select a character animation clip first',{icon:toastIcons.info,duration:1700});return;}
    const p=charParts(c),a=normalizeActionsV128(clip.faceActions);clip.faceActions=a;
    const type=String(c.modelType||(c.sheet&&c.sheet.modelType)||'humanoid'),species=type==='animal'||type==='bird';
    const back=ensureFaceBackdrop(),oldSheet=back.querySelector('.modal-sheet'),oldScroll=oldSheet?oldSheet.scrollTop:0;
    const title=species?'Face &amp; mouth actions':'Model parts &amp; hands';
    const moveNote=species?`<p class="evca-species-movement-note">${type==='bird'?'Wing flaps, hops and bird movement':'Walking, running and four-legged movement'} are controlled by the matching animation presets in the Animations section.</p>`:'';
    const wingSection=(type==='bird'&&(p.wingL_fly||p.wingR_fly))?`<section class="evpa-section evpa-wings-section"><p class="evpa-section-title">Wings</p><div class="evpa-choice-grid">${wingChoiceGrid(c,a)}</div></section>`:'';
    const handSections=species?'':`<section class="evpa-section"><p class="evpa-section-title">Left hand</p><div class="evpa-choice-grid">${handChoiceGrid(c,a,'L')}</div></section><section class="evpa-section"><p class="evpa-section-title">Right hand</p><div class="evpa-choice-grid">${handChoiceGrid(c,a,'R')}</div></section>`;
    back.innerHTML=`<div class="modal-sheet evca-char-actions-sheet" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="${species?'Face and mouth actions':'Model parts and hand gestures'}">
      <div class="sheet-handle"></div><p class="sheet-title">${title}</p>
      <section class="evpa-section"><p class="evpa-section-title">Face expression</p><div class="evpa-choice-grid">${faceChoiceGrid(c,a)}</div></section>
      <section class="evpa-section"><p class="evpa-section-title">Eyes</p><div class="evpa-choice-grid">${eyeChoiceGrid(c,a)}</div></section>
      <section class="evpa-section"><p class="evpa-section-title">Blink mode</p><div class="evpa-choice-grid">${blinkChoiceGrid(a)}</div></section>
      <section class="evpa-section"><p class="evpa-section-title">Mouth</p><div class="evpa-choice-grid">${mouthChoiceGrid(c,a)}</div></section>
      ${mouthItemSection(c)}${handSections}${wingSection}
      <button type="button" class="evpa-close-btn" onclick="closeClipCharacterActions()">Done</button>
    </div>`;
    back.classList.add('active');
    if(oldScroll)requestAnimationFrame(()=>{const sheet=back.querySelector('.modal-sheet');if(sheet)sheet.scrollTop=oldScroll;});
  };
  window.closeClipCharacterActions=function(){const el=$('ethvelleClipFaceActionsBackdrop');if(el)el.classList.remove('active');};
  window.updateClipFaceAction=function(field,value){
    const clip=currentAnimClip(),c=currentChar();if(!clip||!c)return;
    const a=normalizeActionsV128(clip.faceActions);
    const allowed={expression:['none','sad','angry','shock','laugh'],mouth:['preset','talk','closed','hold'],blink:['auto','ambient','off'],eyes:['auto','open','closed'],wings:['natural','fly'],leftHand:['none','point','thumb','palm','fist','hold'],rightHand:['none','point','thumb','palm','fist','hold']};
    if(!allowed[field]||!allowed[field].includes(value))return;
    const partsNow=charParts(c),typeNow=typeOfChar(c);
    const birdKitNow=typeNow==='bird'&&hasBirdKit(partsNow),humKitNow=typeNow==='humanoid'&&hasHumanoidKit(partsNow);
    const animalKit=(typeNow==='animal'&&partsNow.eye_L&&partsNow.eye_R)||humKitNow;
    if(field==='wings'&&(typeNow!=='bird'||(value==='fly'&&!(partsNow.wingL_fly||partsNow.wingR_fly))))return;
    if(field==='expression'&&value!=='none'&&!(birdKitNow||partsNow['head_'+value]))return;
    if(field==='eyes'&&value==='open'&&!(birdKitNow?partsNow.bird_eye:animalKit?(partsNow.eye_L&&partsNow.eye_R):partsNow.head_open))return;
    if(field==='eyes'&&value==='closed'&&!(birdKitNow?partsNow.bird_eye:animalKit?(partsNow.eye_closed_L&&partsNow.eye_closed_R):partsNow.head_blink))return;
    if((field==='leftHand'||field==='rightHand')&&value!=='none'){
      const side=field==='leftHand'?'L':'R';if(!charParts(c)['hand'+side+'_'+value])return;
    }
    clip.faceActions={...a,[field]:value};
    if(field==='mouth'&&(typeNow==='animal'||humKitNow)&&c.expr)c.expr.faceKitHideMouth=value==='closed';
    if(typeof pausePlayback==='function')pausePlayback();
    if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState();
    if(typeof renderFrame==='function')renderFrame();else if(typeof drawStick==='function')drawStick();
    window.openClipCharacterActions();
  };

  window.openSelectedCharacterModelEditor=function(){
    const c=currentChar();if(!c){if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Select a character first',{icon:toastIcons.info});return;}
    if(c.sheet&&typeof edOpenFromChar==='function'){edOpenFromChar(activeCharIdx);return;}
    if(c.type==='custom'&&c.parts&&typeof openCustomCharBuilder==='function'){openCustomCharBuilder(true);return;}
    if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('This character has no editable image parts. Add a 14-part model first.',{icon:toastIcons.info,duration:2300});
  };

  let propSide='R';
  function ensurePropsBackdrop(){
    let el=$('ethvelleCharacterPropsBackdrop');
    if(!el){const host=$('screen-editor')||document.body;el=document.createElement('div');el.id='ethvelleCharacterPropsBackdrop';el.className='modal-backdrop evca-char-actions-backdrop';host.appendChild(el);el.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target===el)closeCharacterPropsPanel();});}
    return el;
  }
  function ensurePropState(c){if(!c.handProps||typeof c.handProps!=='object')c.handProps={L:null,R:null};if(!('L'in c.handProps))c.handProps.L=null;if(!('R'in c.handProps))c.handProps.R=null;return c.handProps;}
  function drawPropsPanelPreview(){
    const target=$('evpaPropStagePreview'),source=$('stickCanvas');
    if(!target||!source||!source.width||!source.height)return;
    const ctx=target.getContext('2d');if(!ctx)return;
    ctx.clearRect(0,0,target.width,target.height);
    const ratio=Math.min(target.width/source.width,target.height/source.height);
    const w=source.width*ratio,h=source.height*ratio;
    try{ctx.drawImage(source,(target.width-w)/2,(target.height-h)/2,w,h);}catch(_e){}
  }
  function refreshPropsPreview(){requestAnimationFrame(drawPropsPanelPreview);}
  function renderPropsPanel(){
    const c=currentChar();if(!c)return;const props=ensurePropState(c),item=props[propSide];
    const preview=item?`<img src="${safe(item.src)}" alt="Held object preview">`:`<span class="evpa-prop-empty">No object</span>`;
    const n=(v,d)=>Number.isFinite(Number(v))?Number(v):d;
    const scale=n(item&&item.scale,1),rotation=n(item&&item.rotation,0),x=n(item&&item.offsetX,0),y=n(item&&item.offsetY,0);
    const back=ensurePropsBackdrop();
    back.innerHTML=`<div class="modal-sheet evpa-props-sheet" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="Held items">
      <div class="sheet-handle"></div><p class="sheet-title">Held items</p>
      <div class="evpa-action-side"><button type="button" class="${propSide==='L'?'active':''}" onclick="setCharacterPropSide('L')">Left hand</button><button type="button" class="${propSide==='R'?'active':''}" onclick="setCharacterPropSide('R')">Right hand</button></div>
      <div class="evpa-prop-preview" id="evpaPropPreview">${preview}</div>
      <input id="evpaPropFile" type="file" accept="image/png,image/webp,image/jpeg,image/*" hidden onchange="setCharacterHeldPropFile(this)">
      <button type="button" class="evpa-prop-upload" onclick="document.getElementById('evpaPropFile').click()">＋ ${item?'Replace object':'Add object image'}</button>
      <div class="evpa-prop-stage-wrap"><div class="evpa-prop-stage-title">On-character preview</div><canvas id="evpaPropStagePreview" width="270" height="480" aria-label="Character with held object preview"></canvas></div>
      <div class="evpa-prop-controls" ${item?'':'style="opacity:.45;pointer-events:none"'}>
        <div class="evpa-prop-field"><label>Size <output id="evpaPropScaleVal">${Math.round(scale*100)}%</output></label><input type="range" min="20" max="250" step="1" value="${Math.round(scale*100)}" oninput="setCharacterHeldPropValue('scale',Number(this.value)/100)"></div>
        <div class="evpa-prop-field"><label>Rotate <output id="evpaPropRotationVal">${Math.round(rotation)}°</output></label><input type="range" min="-180" max="180" step="1" value="${Math.round(rotation)}" oninput="setCharacterHeldPropValue('rotation',Number(this.value))"></div>
        <div class="evpa-prop-field"><label>Move X <output id="evpaPropXVal">${Math.round(x)}</output></label><input type="range" id="evpaPropXInput" min="-100" max="100" step="1" value="${Math.round(x)}" oninput="setCharacterHeldPropValue('offsetX',Number(this.value))"></div>
        <div class="evpa-prop-field"><label>Move Y <output id="evpaPropYVal">${Math.round(y)}</output></label><input type="range" id="evpaPropYInput" min="-100" max="100" step="1" value="${Math.round(y)}" oninput="setCharacterHeldPropValue('offsetY',Number(this.value))"></div>
      </div>
      <div class="evpa-prop-toggle-row" ${item?'':'style="opacity:.45;pointer-events:none"'}><button type="button" class="${item&&item.flipX?'active':''}" onclick="toggleCharacterHeldProp('flipX')">Flip horizontal</button><button type="button" class="${item&&item.flipY?'active':''}" onclick="toggleCharacterHeldProp('flipY')">Flip vertical</button></div>
      ${item?'<button type="button" class="evpa-prop-remove" onclick="removeCharacterHeldProp()">Remove object</button>':''}
      <button type="button" class="evpa-close-btn" onclick="closeCharacterPropsPanel()">Done</button>
    </div>`;
    back.classList.add('active');
    refreshPropsPreview();
  }
  window.openCharacterPropsPanel=function(){
    const c=currentChar();if(!c){if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Select a character first',{icon:toastIcons.info});return;}
    ensurePropState(c);propSide='R';renderPropsPanel();
  };
  window.closeCharacterPropsPanel=function(){const el=$('ethvelleCharacterPropsBackdrop');if(el)el.classList.remove('active');};
  window.setCharacterPropSide=function(side){if(!['L','R'].includes(side))return;propSide=side;renderPropsPanel();};
  window.setCharacterHeldPropValue=function(field,value){
    const c=currentChar(),item=c&&ensurePropState(c)[propSide];if(!item)return;
    if(!['scale','rotation','offsetX','offsetY'].includes(field)||!Number.isFinite(Number(value)))return;
    item[field]=Number(value);
    const output={scale:'evpaPropScaleVal',rotation:'evpaPropRotationVal',offsetX:'evpaPropXVal',offsetY:'evpaPropYVal'}[field];
    const label=$(output);if(label)label.textContent=field==='scale'?Math.round(item.scale*100)+'%':(field==='rotation'?Math.round(item.rotation)+'°':Math.round(item[field]));
    if(typeof drawStick==='function')drawStick();if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState();refreshPropsPreview();
  };
  window.toggleCharacterHeldProp=function(field){
    if(!['flipX','flipY'].includes(field))return;const c=currentChar(),item=c&&ensurePropState(c)[propSide];if(!item)return;
    item[field]=!item[field];if(typeof drawStick==='function')drawStick();if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState();renderPropsPanel();
  };
  window.removeCharacterHeldProp=function(){const c=currentChar();if(!c)return;ensurePropState(c)[propSide]=null;if(typeof drawStick==='function')drawStick();if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState();renderPropsPanel();};
  window.setCharacterHeldPropFile=function(input){
    const file=input.files&&input.files[0];input.value='';if(!file)return;
    const c=currentChar();if(!c)return;
    const reader=new FileReader();reader.onerror=()=>{if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Could not read the object image',{icon:toastIcons.info});};
    reader.onload=()=>{
      const source=String(reader.result||'');const img=new Image();
      img.onerror=()=>{if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Could not decode the object image',{icon:toastIcons.info});};
      img.onload=()=>{
        const max=640,ratio=Math.min(1,max/Math.max(img.naturalWidth,img.naturalHeight));
        const w=Math.max(1,Math.round(img.naturalWidth*ratio)),h=Math.max(1,Math.round(img.naturalHeight*ratio));
        const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');canvas.width=w;canvas.height=h;
        const cx=canvas.getContext('2d');if(!cx){if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Object image could not be prepared',{icon:toastIcons.info});return;}
        cx.clearRect(0,0,w,h);cx.drawImage(img,0,0,w,h);
        const data=canvas.toDataURL('image/png');
        ensurePropState(c)[propSide]={src:data,name:file.name||'Object',scale:1,rotation:0,offsetX:0,offsetY:0,flipX:false,flipY:false};
        const clip=currentAnimClip(),parts=charParts(c),field=propSide==='L'?'leftHand':'rightHand';
        if(clip&&parts['hand'+propSide+'_hold']){const a=normalizeActionsV128(clip.faceActions);clip.faceActions={...a,[field]:'hold'};}
        if(typeof drawStick==='function')drawStick();if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState();renderPropsPanel();
        if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Object attached to '+(propSide==='L'?'left':'right')+' hand',{icon:toastIcons.check,duration:1400});
      };
      img.src=source;
    };
    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
  };

  // Draw an uploaded object over its hand in the same local bone-space transform as the character.
  // Because drawCharacterToContext calls this hook, the object is visible both on the editor stage and export frames.
  window.ethvelleDrawCharacterProps=function(targetCtx,char,boneStart,boneEnd){
    const props=char&&char.handProps;if(!props)return;
    const cache=window.__ethvelleHeldPropImageCache||(window.__ethvelleHeldPropImageCache=new Map());
    ['L','R'].forEach(side=>{
      const item=props[side];if(!item||!item.src)return;
      const key='hand'+side;let img=cache.get(item.src);
      if(!img){img=new Image();img.onload=()=>{if(typeof drawStick==='function')drawStick();};img.onerror=()=>{};cache.set(item.src,img);img.src=item.src;}
      if(!img.complete||!img.naturalWidth)return;
      let start,end;try{start=boneStart(key);end=boneEnd(key);}catch(_e){return;}
      if(!start||!end||![start.x,start.y,end.x,end.y].every(Number.isFinite))return;
      const dx=end.x-start.x,dy=end.y-start.y,angle=Math.atan2(dy,dx)-Math.PI/2+(Number(item.rotation)||0)*Math.PI/180;
      const handX=start.x+dx*.52,handY=start.y+dy*.52;
      const bodyKeys=['spine_lower','spine_upper','chest','neck','thighL','shinL','thighR','shinR'];
      const bodyLen=bodyKeys.reduce((sum,k)=>sum+Math.max(0,Number(char.bones&&char.bones[k]&&char.bones[k].length)||0),0)||Number(stageH)||420;
      const h=Math.max(24,Math.min(190,bodyLen*.235*Math.max(.2,Math.min(2.5,Number(item.scale)||1))));
      const aspect=img.naturalWidth/Math.max(1,img.naturalHeight),w=Math.max(16,Math.min(190,h*aspect));
      targetCtx.save();targetCtx.translate(handX,handY);targetCtx.rotate(angle);
      targetCtx.translate(Number(item.offsetX)||0,Number(item.offsetY)||0);
      targetCtx.scale(item.flipX?-1:1,item.flipY?-1:1);
      targetCtx.drawImage(img,-w*.5,-h*.55,w,h);
      targetCtx.restore();
    });
  };

  // Force the latest contextual toolbar state after every existing synchronizer (many versions wrap it).
  const priorSync=window.syncBottomToolsMode;
  if(typeof priorSync==='function'&&!priorSync.__ethvelleV128ModelProps){
    const wrapped=function(){const r=priorSync.apply(this,arguments);const isStick=!!currentAnimClip();
      const face=$('clipCharacterActionsTool'),model=$('clipModelPartsTool'),held=$('clipHeldItemsTool');
      if(face)face.style.display=isStick?'flex':'none';if(model)model.style.display=isStick?'flex':'none';if(held)held.style.display=isStick?'flex':'none';return r;};
    wrapped.__ethvelleV128ModelProps=true;window.syncBottomToolsMode=wrapped;
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>{if(window.syncBottomToolsMode)window.syncBottomToolsMode();},{once:true});
  else if(window.syncBottomToolsMode)window.syncBottomToolsMode();
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v129-canvas-eyedropper-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const EYEDROP_TOUCH_OFFSET_Y=0;
  const STORE_KEY='ethvelleCustomTextColors';
  const VALID_KEYS=new Set(['textColor','strokeColor','shadowColor','textBackgroundColor']);
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const clamp=(v,min,max)=>Math.max(min,Math.min(max,v));
  let session=null, dockObserver=null, chromaObserver=null, bodyObserver=null, uiRaf=0;
  let observedDock=null, observedChroma=null, swallowClickUntil=0, swallowClickPoint=null, historyArmed=false, ignoreNextPop=false;
  let loupe=null;

  function toast(message){try{if(typeof window.showToast==='function')window.showToast(message);}catch(_e){}}
  function normalizedHex(value){return /^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(String(value||''))?String(value).toUpperCase():null;}
  function readSavedColors(){
    try{
      const raw=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(STORE_KEY)||'[]');if(!Array.isArray(raw))return [];
      const seen=new Set(),out=[];
      raw.forEach(v=>{const h=normalizedHex(v);if(h&&!seen.has(h)){seen.add(h);out.push(h);}});
      return out.slice(0,30);
    }catch(_e){return [];}
  }
  function saveColor(hex){
    try{
      const next=[...readSavedColors().filter(c=>c!==hex),hex].slice(-30);
      localStorage.setItem(STORE_KEY,JSON.stringify(next));
      return next;
    }catch(_e){return readSavedColors();}
  }
  function eyeMarkup(){
    return '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m2 22 1-1h3l9-9"/><path d="M3 21v-3l9-9"/><path d="m15 6 3.4-3.4a2.1 2.1 0 1 1 3 3L18 9l.4.4a2.1 2.1 0 1 1-3 3l-3.8-3.8a2.1 2.1 0 1 1 3-3l.4.4Z"/></svg>';
  }
  function makeEye(mode){
    const b=document.createElement('button');b.type='button';
    b.className='ev-eyedrop-button ev-eyedrop-'+mode;b.dataset.evEyedropButton=mode;
    b.setAttribute('aria-label','Pick color from canvas');b.setAttribute('aria-pressed','false');b.title='Pick color from canvas';
    b.innerHTML=eyeMarkup();return b;
  }
  function makeTextSwatch(hex){
    const b=document.createElement('button');b.type='button';b.className='evpa-swatch';b.dataset.color=hex;
    b.style.backgroundColor=hex;b.title=hex;b.setAttribute('aria-label','Text colour '+hex);return b;
  }
  function ensureTextPalette(){
    const dock=$('ethvellePicsartTextDock'),palette=$('evpaPalette');
    if(!dock||!palette)return;
    if(dock.classList.contains('evpa-in-composer')||$('ethvelleTextComposer')?.classList.contains('active')){
      dock.querySelectorAll('.ev-eyedrop-text').forEach(b=>b.remove());return;
    }
    const wheels=[...palette.querySelectorAll('.evpa-color-wheel')];
    if(!wheels.length)return;
    dock.querySelectorAll('.evpa-eye').forEach(el=>el.remove());
    const saved=readSavedColors();
    const known=new Set([...palette.querySelectorAll('.evpa-swatch[data-color]')].map(b=>normalizedHex(b.dataset.color)).filter(Boolean));
    wheels.forEach(wheel=>{
      const parent=wheel.parentElement;if(!parent)return;
      let eye=parent.querySelector(':scope > .ev-eyedrop-text');
      if(!eye){eye=makeEye('text');parent.insertBefore(eye,wheel);}else if(eye.nextElementSibling!==wheel){parent.insertBefore(eye,wheel);}
    });
    // v219-fix: a saved (picked) colour that is missing from the row is inserted directly after Transparent
    // (newest first), in the same container as Transparent, instead of at the far end of the row.
    const clear=palette.querySelector('.evpa-transparent');
    if(clear&&clear.parentElement){
      let anchor=clear;
      [...saved].reverse().forEach(color=>{
        if(known.has(color))return;
        const sw=makeTextSwatch(color);anchor.after(sw);anchor=sw;known.add(color);
      });
    }
    if(typeof window.ethvelleRefreshDockSwatches==='function'){
      try{window.ethvelleRefreshDockSwatches();}catch(_e){}
    }
  }
  function ensureChromaPalette(){
    const host=$('evltChromaPalette');if(!host)return;
    const custom=host.querySelector('.evlt-chroma-custom');if(!custom)return;
    let eye=host.querySelector(':scope > .ev-eyedrop-chroma');if(!eye)eye=makeEye('chroma');
    const swatches=[...host.querySelectorAll('.evlt-chroma-swatch')];
    const saved=readSavedColors(),savedNodes=[];
    saved.forEach(color=>{
      let matches=swatches.filter(b=>normalizedHex(b.dataset.color)===color);
      if(matches[0])savedNodes.push(matches[0]);
    });
    const reserved=new Set([eye,custom,...savedNodes]);
    const rest=[...host.children].filter(el=>!reserved.has(el));
    const desired=[eye,custom,...savedNodes,...rest];
    desired.forEach((el,index)=>{const at=host.children[index]||null;if(at!==el)host.insertBefore(el,at);});
  }
  window.__ev226EnsureChromaPalette=ensureChromaPalette;
  function ensureUI(){
    ensureTextPalette();ensureChromaPalette();
    if(session){
      const button=session.mode==='text'?document.querySelector('#ethvellePicsartTextDock .ev-eyedrop-text'):document.querySelector('#evltChromaPalette .ev-eyedrop-chroma');
      if(button)button.setAttribute('aria-pressed','true');
    }
  }
  function queueUI(){if(uiRaf)return;uiRaf=requestAnimationFrame(()=>{uiRaf=0;ensureUI();checkSessionContext();});}
  function attachObservers(){
    const dock=$('ethvellePicsartTextDock');
    if(dock&&dock!==observedDock){if(dockObserver)dockObserver.disconnect();observedDock=dock;dockObserver=new MutationObserver(queueUI);dockObserver.observe(dock,{childList:true,subtree:true,attributes:true,attributeFilter:['class','style']});}
    const chroma=$('chromaPanelBody');
    if(chroma&&chroma!==observedChroma){if(chromaObserver)chromaObserver.disconnect();observedChroma=chroma;chromaObserver=new MutationObserver(queueUI);chromaObserver.observe(chroma,{childList:true,subtree:true,attributes:true,attributeFilter:['class','style']});}
    queueUI();
  }
  function ensureLoupe(){
    if(loupe&&loupe.isConnected)return loupe;
    loupe=$('evEyedropLoupe');
    if(!loupe){loupe=document.createElement('div');loupe.id='evEyedropLoupe';loupe.innerHTML='<span class="ev-eyedrop-crosshair" aria-hidden="true"></span>';document.body.appendChild(loupe);}
    return loupe;
  }
  function isVisible(el){
    if(!el||!el.isConnected)return false;
    const r=el.getBoundingClientRect(),cs=getComputedStyle(el);
    return cs.display!=='none'&&cs.visibility!=='hidden'&&r.width>0&&r.height>0;
  }
  function visibleCanvasRect(mode){
    const stage=$('canvasStage');if(!stage||!isVisible(stage))return null;
    const r=stage.getBoundingClientRect();let bottom=r.bottom;
    const panel=mode==='text' ? $('ethvellePicsartTextDock') : $('chromaPanelBody');
    if(isVisible(panel)){
      const p=panel.getBoundingClientRect();
      if(p.top<bottom&&p.bottom>r.top&&p.left<r.right&&p.right>r.left)bottom=Math.min(bottom,p.top);
    }
    const out={left:r.left,top:r.top,right:r.right,bottom,width:r.width,height:Math.max(0,bottom-r.top)};
    return out.width>0&&out.height>0?out:null;
  }
  function insideRect(x,y,r){return !!r&&x>=r.left&&x<=r.right&&y>=r.top&&y<=r.bottom;}
  function isCanvasPointerTarget(target){
    const stage=$('canvasStage');if(!stage||!target||!(target===stage||stage.contains(target)))return false;
    return !(target.closest&&target.closest('button,input,select,textarea,[role="button"],.evca-inline-zoom-hud,.evca-zoom-control-bar'));
  }
  function activeTab(){return $('evpaTabs')?.querySelector('.evpa-tab.active')?.dataset.tab||'';}
  function textColorKey(){
    let key='';try{key=typeof window.ethvellePicsartGetColorTarget==='function'?window.ethvellePicsartGetColorTarget():'';}catch(_e){}
    if(VALID_KEYS.has(key))return key;
    const tab=activeTab();return tab==='stroke'?'strokeColor':tab==='shadow'?'shadowColor':(tab==='highlight'||tab==='background')?'textBackgroundColor':'textColor';
  }
  function parseRgb(css){
    const m=String(css||'').match(/rgba?\(\s*([\d.]+)[,\s]+([\d.]+)[,\s]+([\d.]+)(?:\s*[,/]\s*([\d.]+%?))?\s*\)/i);
    if(!m)return [255,255,255,1];
    const alpha=m[4]?(m[4].endsWith('%')?Number(m[4].slice(0,-1))/100:Number(m[4])):1;
    return [Number(m[1]),Number(m[2]),Number(m[3])].map(v=>clamp(v,0,255)).concat(clamp(alpha,0,1));
  }
  function makeSnapshot(mode,clip){
    if(typeof window.ethvelleFrameCanvas!=='function')throw new Error('Frame compositor is unavailable');
    if(mode==='chroma'){
      if(!clip)throw new Error('Select a video clip first');
      if(!clip.chromaKey)clip.chromaKey={enabled:false,color:'#00FF00',tolerance:75,softness:24};
    }
    // Chroma mode samples the un-keyed picture, so keying is switched off only while the frame is captured.
    const capture=opts=>{
      if(mode!=='chroma')return window.ethvelleFrameCanvas(opts);
      const previous=clip.chromaKey.enabled;
      try{clip.chromaKey.enabled=false;return window.ethvelleFrameCanvas(opts);}
      finally{clip.chromaKey.enabled=previous;}
    };
    const build=opts=>{
      const frame=capture(opts);
      if(!frame||!frame.width||!frame.height)throw new Error('Canvas frame is unavailable');
      const copy=document.createElement('canvas');copy.width=frame.width;copy.height=frame.height;
      const ctx=copy.getContext('2d',{willReadFrequently:true});if(!ctx)throw new Error('Pixel reader is unavailable');
      ctx.drawImage(frame,0,0);
      ctx.getImageData(0,0,1,1); // throws if any source tainted the canvas
      return {canvas:copy,ctx};
    };
    let snap;
    try{snap=build();}
    catch(err){
      // A remote image/video without CORS blocks pixel reads. Retry without those sources
      // so the picker still samples the clips and layers it is allowed to read.
      try{snap=build({safe:true});}
      finally{if(typeof window.renderFrame==='function')try{window.renderFrame();}catch(_e){}}
    }
    if(typeof window.renderFrame==='function')window.renderFrame();
    return snap;
  }
  function sampleAt(x,y){
    const stage=$('canvasStage'),r=stage&&stage.getBoundingClientRect(),snap=session&&session.snapshot;
    if(!r||!snap||!r.width||!r.height)throw new Error('Canvas is unavailable');
    const px=clamp(Math.floor((x-r.left)/r.width*snap.canvas.width),0,snap.canvas.width-1);
    const py=clamp(Math.floor((y-r.top)/r.height*snap.canvas.height),0,snap.canvas.height-1);
    const data=snap.ctx.getImageData(px,py,1,1).data;
    let rr=data[0],gg=data[1],bb=data[2],aa=data[3]/255;
    if(aa<1){
      const bg=parseRgb(getComputedStyle(stage).backgroundColor),ba=bg[3];
      const br=bg[0]*ba+255*(1-ba),bgc=bg[1]*ba+255*(1-ba),bbg=bg[2]*ba+255*(1-ba);
      rr=rr*aa+br*(1-aa);gg=gg*aa+bgc*(1-aa);bb=bb*aa+bbg*(1-aa);
    }
    return '#'+[rr,gg,bb].map(v=>Math.round(clamp(v,0,255)).toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('').toUpperCase();
  }
  function moveLoupe(x,y){
    const r=visibleCanvasRect(session.mode);if(!r)return false;
    const px=clamp(x,r.left,r.right),py=clamp(y+EYEDROP_TOUCH_OFFSET_Y,r.top,r.bottom);
    const color=sampleAt(px,py),el=ensureLoupe();
    el.style.setProperty('--ev-eyedrop-color',color);el.style.left=px+'px';el.style.top=py+'px';el.style.display='block';
    session.point={x:px,y:py};session.color=color;return true;
  }
  function textOpacityKey(key){return key==='textColor'?'textOpacity':key==='strokeColor'?'strokeOpacity':key==='shadowColor'?'shadowOpacity':'textBackgroundOpacity';}
  function applyTextColor(hex,key){
    const target=typeof window.ethvellePicsartGetTarget==='function'?window.ethvellePicsartGetTarget():null;
    if(!target){toast('Select a text layer first');return;}
    const opacityKey=textOpacityKey(key),wasTransparent=Number(target[opacityKey])===0;
    if(typeof window.ethvellePicsartSetColorTarget==='function')window.ethvellePicsartSetColorTarget(key);
    if(typeof window.ethvellePicsartSetColor==='function')window.ethvellePicsartSetColor(key,hex);
    if(wasTransparent&&typeof window.ethvellePicsartSetValue==='function')window.ethvellePicsartSetValue(opacityKey,100);
    saveColor(hex);
    if(typeof window.renderFrame==='function')window.renderFrame();
    if(typeof window.ethvelleRefreshDockSwatches==='function')window.ethvelleRefreshDockSwatches();
    ensureTextPalette();
    const palette=$('evpaPalette');
    palette?.querySelectorAll('.evpa-swatch[data-color]').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('active',normalizedHex(b.dataset.color)===hex));
    try{const on=palette&&palette.querySelector('.evpa-swatch.active[data-color]');if(on&&on.scrollIntoView)on.scrollIntoView({block:'nearest',inline:'nearest',behavior:'auto'});}catch(_e){}
  }
  function applyChromaColor(hex,clip){
    const current=typeof window.getSelectedVideoOverlay==='function'?window.getSelectedVideoOverlay():null;
    if(!current||String(current.id)!==String(clip&&clip.id)){toast('Select a video clip first');return;}
    saveColor(hex);
    if(typeof window.ethvelleSetChromaPaletteColor==='function')window.ethvelleSetChromaPaletteColor(hex,null);
    else if(typeof window.updateChromaSetting==='function')window.updateChromaSetting('color',hex);
    if(typeof window.renderChromaKeyPanel==='function')window.renderChromaKeyPanel();
    const preview=$('evltChromaCurrent');if(preview)preview.style.background=hex;
    ensureChromaPalette();
    $('evltChromaPalette')?.querySelectorAll('.evlt-chroma-swatch').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('active',normalizedHex(b.dataset.color)===hex));
  }
  function removeActiveListeners(){
    if(session&&session.moveRaf)cancelAnimationFrame(session.moveRaf);
    window.removeEventListener('pointerdown',onPointerDown,true);
    window.removeEventListener('pointermove',onPointerMove,true);
    window.removeEventListener('pointerup',onPointerUp,true);
    window.removeEventListener('pointercancel',onPointerCancel,true);
    window.removeEventListener('keydown',onKeyDown,true);
  }
  function clearHistoryEntry(){
    if(historyArmed){historyArmed=false;ignoreNextPop=true;try{history.back();}catch(_e){ignoreNextPop=false;}}
  }
  function cleanup(callCancel,fromPop){
    if(!session)return;
    const old=session;if(old.moveRaf)cancelAnimationFrame(old.moveRaf);session=null;removeActiveListeners();
    document.documentElement.classList.remove('ev-eyedrop-active');
    if(loupe)loupe.style.display='none';
    document.querySelectorAll('.ev-eyedrop-button').forEach(b=>b.setAttribute('aria-pressed','false'));
    if(!fromPop)clearHistoryEntry();
    if(callCancel&&typeof old.onCancel==='function'){try{old.onCancel();}catch(_e){}}
  }
  function cancel(){cleanup(true,false);}
  function failRead(){toast('This frame cannot be read for color picking');cleanup(true,false);}
  function finish(){
    if(!session)return;
    const old=session,hex=old.color;
    cleanup(false,false);
    armSwallowClick(old.point&&old.point.x,old.point&&old.point.y);
    if(hex&&typeof old.onPick==='function'){try{old.onPick(hex);}catch(err){console.error('Ethvelle eyedropper apply failed',err);}}
  }
  function checkSessionContext(){
    if(!session)return;
    if(session.mode==='text'){
      const dock=$('ethvellePicsartTextDock');
      if(!isVisible(dock)||dock.classList.contains('evpa-in-composer')||$('ethvelleTextComposer')?.classList.contains('active')||activeTab()!==session.tab){cancel();return;}
    }else{
      const panel=$('chromaPanelBody');
      const clip=typeof window.getSelectedVideoOverlay==='function'?window.getSelectedVideoOverlay():null;
      if(!isVisible(panel)||!clip||String(clip.id)!==String(session.clip.id)){cancel();return;}
    }
  }
  function start(options){
    options=options||{};const mode=options.mode==='chroma'?'chroma':'text';
    if(session)cancel();
    const stage=$('canvasStage'),rect=visibleCanvasRect(mode);
    if(!stage||!rect){toast('Canvas is not available');return false;}
    let clip=null,key=null;
    if(mode==='text'&&($('ethvellePicsartTextDock')?.classList.contains('evpa-in-composer')||$('ethvelleTextComposer')?.classList.contains('active')))return false;
    if(mode==='chroma'){
      clip=typeof window.getSelectedVideoOverlay==='function'?window.getSelectedVideoOverlay():null;
      if(!clip){toast('Select a video clip first');return false;}
    }else{
      key=textColorKey();
      if(typeof window.ethvellePicsartGetTarget==='function'&&!window.ethvellePicsartGetTarget()){toast('Select a text layer first');return false;}
    }
    try{if(typeof window.pausePlayback==='function')window.pausePlayback();}catch(_e){}
    session={mode,clip,key,tab:activeTab(),onPick:options.onPick,onCancel:options.onCancel,pointerId:null,point:null,color:'#777777',snapshot:null};
    try{
      session.snapshot=makeSnapshot(mode,clip);
      document.documentElement.classList.add('ev-eyedrop-active');
      const x=(rect.left+rect.right)/2,y=(rect.top+rect.bottom)/2;
      moveLoupe(x,y);
      ensureLoupe().style.display='block';
      window.addEventListener('pointerdown',onPointerDown,{capture:true,passive:false});
      window.addEventListener('pointermove',onPointerMove,{capture:true,passive:false});
      window.addEventListener('pointerup',onPointerUp,{capture:true,passive:false});
      window.addEventListener('pointercancel',onPointerCancel,{capture:true,passive:false});
      window.addEventListener('keydown',onKeyDown,true);
      try{history.pushState(Object.assign({},history.state||{},{evEyedropperActive:true}),document.title,location.href);historyArmed=true;}catch(_e){}
      ensureUI();return true;
    }catch(err){
      if(err&&(/security|taint|read|pixel/i.test(String(err.name||'')+' '+String(err.message||''))))toast('This frame cannot be read for color picking');
      else toast('This frame cannot be read for color picking');
      cleanup(true,false);return false;
    }
  }
  function swallow(e){if(e.cancelable)e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();e.stopImmediatePropagation();}
  function armSwallowClick(x,y){swallowClickUntil=performance.now()+450;swallowClickPoint=Number.isFinite(Number(x))&&Number.isFinite(Number(y))?{x:Number(x),y:Number(y)}:null;}
  function consumeSwallowClick(e){
    if(!swallowClickUntil)return false;
    const near=swallowClickPoint&&Math.hypot((Number(e.clientX)||0)-swallowClickPoint.x,(Number(e.clientY)||0)-swallowClickPoint.y)<=32;
    if(performance.now()<=swallowClickUntil&&near){swallowClickUntil=0;swallowClickPoint=null;swallow(e);return true;}
    if(performance.now()>swallowClickUntil){swallowClickUntil=0;swallowClickPoint=null;}
    return false;
  }
  function onPointerDown(e){
    if(!session)return;
    if(e.target?.closest?.('.ev-eyedrop-button')){armSwallowClick(e.clientX,e.clientY);cancel();return;}
    if(session.pointerId!==null){swallow(e);return;}
    const rect=visibleCanvasRect(session.mode);
    if(!insideRect(e.clientX,e.clientY,rect)||!isCanvasPointerTarget(e.target)){cancel();return;}
    swallow(e);session.pointerId=e.pointerId;
    try{if(!moveLoupe(e.clientX,e.clientY))cancel();}catch(_e){failRead();}
  }
  function onPointerMove(e){
    if(!session||session.pointerId!==e.pointerId)return;
    swallow(e);
    session.pendingPoint={x:e.clientX,y:e.clientY};
    if(!session.moveRaf)session.moveRaf=requestAnimationFrame(()=>{
      if(!session)return;const point=session.pendingPoint;session.moveRaf=0;session.pendingPoint=null;
      if(!point)return;try{if(!moveLoupe(point.x,point.y))cancel();}catch(_e){failRead();}
    });
  }
  function onPointerUp(e){
    if(!session||session.pointerId!==e.pointerId)return;
    swallow(e);
    const rect=visibleCanvasRect(session.mode);
    if(session.moveRaf){cancelAnimationFrame(session.moveRaf);session.moveRaf=0;session.pendingPoint=null;}
    if(!insideRect(e.clientX,e.clientY,rect)){cancel();return;}
    try{if(!moveLoupe(e.clientX,e.clientY)){cancel();return;}finish();}catch(_e){failRead();}
  }
  function onPointerCancel(e){if(session&&session.pointerId===e.pointerId){swallow(e);cancel();}}
  function onKeyDown(e){if(e.key==='Escape'||e.key==='BrowserBack'){swallow(e);cancel();}}
  function onPopState(){if(ignoreNextPop){ignoreNextPop=false;return;}if(session){historyArmed=false;cleanup(true,true);}}
  function startText(){
    const key=textColorKey();
    return start({mode:'text',onPick:hex=>applyTextColor(hex,key)});
  }
  function startChroma(){
    const clip=typeof window.getSelectedVideoOverlay==='function'?window.getSelectedVideoOverlay():null;
    if(!clip){toast('Select a video clip first');return false;}
    return start({mode:'chroma',onPick:hex=>applyChromaColor(hex,clip)});
  }
  window.ethvelleCanvasEyedropper={start,cancel,isActive:()=>!!session};
  window.ethvellePicsartSampleColor=startText;
  window.ethvelleStartStageColorSample=startText;

  window.addEventListener('click',function(e){
    const eye=e.target?.closest?.('.ev-eyedrop-text,.ev-eyedrop-chroma');
    if(consumeSwallowClick(e))return;
    if(!eye)return;
    swallow(e);
    if(session){cancel();return;}
    if(eye.classList.contains('ev-eyedrop-chroma'))startChroma();else startText();
  },true);
  ensureLoupe();
  window.addEventListener('popstate',onPopState,true);
  if(typeof MutationObserver==='function'&&document.body){
    bodyObserver=new MutationObserver(()=>attachObservers());
    bodyObserver.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',attachObservers,{once:true});
  else attachObservers();
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v130-timeline-edge-autoscroll">
  // One shared auto-scroll controller for clip moves on Character, Background, Clips,
  // Text and Audio rows. The scroll delta is folded into the drag delta, keeping the
  // dragged clip directly under the finger while the timeline content moves underneath.
  let timelineEdgeAutoScrollRaf = 0;
  let timelineEdgeAutoScrollState = null;

  function timelineDragDeltaPixels(dragState, clientX){
    if(!dragState)return 0;
    const scroller=document.getElementById('timelineScroll');
    const startX=Number(dragState.startX)||0;
    const startScroll=Number.isFinite(Number(dragState.startScrollLeft))?Number(dragState.startScrollLeft):(scroller?Number(scroller.scrollLeft)||0:0);
    const currentScroll=scroller?Number(scroller.scrollLeft)||0:startScroll;
    return (Number(clientX)-startX)+(currentScroll-startScroll);
  }

  function ensureTimelineRoomForDraggedClip(clip){
    if(!clip)return;
    const end=Number(clip.trimEnd);
    if(!Number.isFinite(end))return;
    const currentMax=Math.max(Number(timelineDuration)||2,(typeof totalProjectDuration==='function'?Number(totalProjectDuration())||0:0));
    if(end<currentMax-1.5)return;
    const nextDuration=Math.max(Number(timelineDuration)||2,Math.ceil(end+5));
    if(nextDuration<=(Number(timelineDuration)||0)+0.001)return;
    timelineDuration=nextDuration;
    if(typeof syncTimelineInnerWidth==='function')syncTimelineInnerWidth();
    if(typeof renderRuler==='function')renderRuler();
    if(typeof updateTimeReadout==='function')updateTimeReadout();
  }

  function stopTimelineEdgeAutoScroll(pointerId){
    const state=timelineEdgeAutoScrollState;
    if(state&&pointerId!==undefined&&pointerId!==null&&state.pointerId!==pointerId)return;
    timelineEdgeAutoScrollState=null;
    if(timelineEdgeAutoScrollRaf){cancelAnimationFrame(timelineEdgeAutoScrollRaf);timelineEdgeAutoScrollRaf=0;}
  }

  function runTimelineEdgeAutoScroll(){
    timelineEdgeAutoScrollRaf=0;
    const state=timelineEdgeAutoScrollState;
    const scroller=document.getElementById('timelineScroll');
    if(!state||!state.enabled||!scroller||!scroller.isConnected)return;
    const rect=scroller.getBoundingClientRect();
    const zone=Math.min(76,Math.max(42,rect.width*0.13));
    let step=0;
    if(state.clientX>rect.right-zone){
      const strength=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(state.clientX-(rect.right-zone))/zone));
      step=Math.ceil(1+7*strength);
    }else if(state.clientX<rect.left+zone){
      const strength=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,((rect.left+zone)-state.clientX)/zone));
      step=-Math.ceil(1+7*strength);
    }
    if(step){
      const maxScroll=Math.max(0,scroller.scrollWidth-scroller.clientWidth);
      const before=Number(scroller.scrollLeft)||0;
      const after=Math.max(0,Math.min(maxScroll,before+step));
      if(Math.abs(after-before)>0.25){
        // The auto-scroll is part of moving the clip, not a playhead scrub gesture.
        if(typeof timelineScrollOwner!=='undefined')timelineScrollOwner='programmatic';
        scroller.scrollLeft=after;
        try{if(typeof state.update==='function')state.update();}catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle timeline auto-scroll update failed',err);}
      }
    }
    if(timelineEdgeAutoScrollState===state)timelineEdgeAutoScrollRaf=requestAnimationFrame(runTimelineEdgeAutoScroll);
  }

  function startTimelineEdgeAutoScroll(pointerId,clientX,update){
    stopTimelineEdgeAutoScroll();
    timelineEdgeAutoScrollState={pointerId,clientX:Number(clientX)||0,update,enabled:false};
  }

  function updateTimelineEdgeAutoScroll(pointerId,clientX,update){
    const state=timelineEdgeAutoScrollState;
    if(!state||state.pointerId!==pointerId)return;
    state.clientX=Number(clientX)||0;
    if(typeof update==='function')state.update=update;
    state.enabled=true;
    if(!timelineEdgeAutoScrollRaf)timelineEdgeAutoScrollRaf=requestAnimationFrame(runTimelineEdgeAutoScroll);
  }
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v130-character-object-ui-runtime">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const safe=v=>String(v==null?'':v).replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
  const activeChar=()=>typeof activeCharacter==='function'?activeCharacter():null;
  const propState=c=>{
    if(!c.handProps||typeof c.handProps!=='object')c.handProps={L:null,R:null};
    if(!('L'in c.handProps))c.handProps.L=null;
    if(!('R'in c.handProps))c.handProps.R=null;
    return c.handProps;
  };
  let propSide='R';
  let propSideTouched=false;
  let heldPreviewView=null;
  let heldPreviewDrag=null;
  let heldPreviewRaf=0;
  let heldPreviewOffscreen=null;

  function heldBackdrop(){
    let el=$('ethvelleCharacterPropsBackdrop');
    if(!el){
      const host=$('screen-editor')||document.body;
      el=document.createElement('div');el.id='ethvelleCharacterPropsBackdrop';el.className='modal-backdrop evca-char-actions-backdrop';host.appendChild(el);
      el.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target===el)window.closeCharacterPropsPanel();});
    }
    return el;
  }
  function heldBoneGeometry(c){
    const bones=c&&c.bones||{};const starts=Object.create(null),ends=Object.create(null);
    function start(k){
      if(starts[k])return starts[k];const b=bones[k];if(!b)return null;
      if(!b.parent)return starts[k]={x:Number(b.x)||0,y:Number(b.y)||0};
      const pe=end(b.parent);if(!pe)return null;
      if(b.attachAt!==undefined){const ps=start(b.parent);return starts[k]={x:ps.x+(pe.x-ps.x)*b.attachAt,y:ps.y+(pe.y-ps.y)*b.attachAt};}
      return starts[k]=pe;
    }
    function end(k){
      if(ends[k])return ends[k];const b=bones[k];if(!b)return null;
      if(!b.parent)return ends[k]={x:Number(b.x)||0,y:Number(b.y)||0};
      const pe=end(b.parent);if(!pe)return null;let sx=pe.x,sy=pe.y;
      if(b.attachAt!==undefined){const ps=start(b.parent);sx=ps.x+(pe.x-ps.x)*b.attachAt;sy=ps.y+(pe.y-ps.y)*b.attachAt;}
      return ends[k]={x:sx+Math.cos(Number(b.angle)||0)*(Number(b.length)||0),y:sy+Math.sin(Number(b.angle)||0)*(Number(b.length)||0)};
    }
    return {start,end};
  }
  function heldTransformPoint(p,c){
    const b=c.bones,mt=c.modelTransform||{},anchor=b.hip||{x:0,y:0};
    const mx=c.mirrorX?-1:1,my=c.mirrorY?-1:1,scale=Number(mt.scale)||1,rot=(Number(mt.rotation)||0)*Math.PI/180;
    const x=(p.x-anchor.x)*mx*scale,y=(p.y-anchor.y)*my*scale;
    return {x:anchor.x+(Number(mt.offsetX)||0)+x*Math.cos(rot)-y*Math.sin(rot),y:anchor.y+(Number(mt.offsetY)||0)+x*Math.sin(rot)+y*Math.cos(rot)};
  }
  function drawHeldCharacterPreview(){
    const target=$('evpaPropStagePreview'),c=activeChar();
    if(!target||!c||!c.bones||typeof drawCharacterToContext!=='function')return;
    const ctx=target.getContext('2d');if(!ctx)return;
    const W=Number(typeof stageW!=='undefined'?stageW:660)||660,H=Number(typeof stageH!=='undefined'?stageH:1170)||1170;
    /* Render once into the final high-density preview canvas. Drawing directly avoids
       an intermediate bitmap resize, which was softening small character-part details. */

    /* Include every bone, including both legs and feet: the old upper-body-only crop hid the full model. */
    const geo=heldBoneGeometry(c),pts=[];
    Object.keys(c.bones).forEach(k=>{
      const a=geo.start(k),b=geo.end(k);
      if(a&&Number.isFinite(a.x)&&Number.isFinite(a.y))pts.push(heldTransformPoint(a,c));
      if(b&&Number.isFinite(b.x)&&Number.isFinite(b.y))pts.push(heldTransformPoint(b,c));
    });
    if(!pts.length){const a=c.bones.hip||{x:W/2,y:H/2};pts.push(heldTransformPoint(a,c));}
    /* Animals and birds: the head, tail, paws and wings extend beyond the bone endpoints, so frame the real artwork bounds. */
    let speciesBounds=null;
    try{const api=window.__ethvelleV264SpeciesBoundsApi;speciesBounds=api&&typeof api.measure==='function'?api.measure(c):null;}catch(_e){}
    if(speciesBounds)[[speciesBounds.minX,speciesBounds.minY],[speciesBounds.maxX,speciesBounds.minY],[speciesBounds.maxX,speciesBounds.maxY],[speciesBounds.minX,speciesBounds.maxY]].forEach(q=>pts.push(heldTransformPoint({x:q[0],y:q[1]},c)));
    let x0=Math.min(...pts.map(p=>p.x)),x1=Math.max(...pts.map(p=>p.x)),y0=Math.min(...pts.map(p=>p.y)),y1=Math.max(...pts.map(p=>p.y));
    const bh=Math.max(100,y1-y0),bw=Math.max(80,x1-x0);
    /* Tight but safe padding makes the full-body character substantially larger in the frame. */
    const padX=Math.max(34,bw*.18,bh*.05),padY=Math.max(48,bh*.17);
    let sx=speciesBounds?x0-padX:Math.max(0,x0-padX),sy=speciesBounds?y0-padY:Math.max(0,y0-padY),ex=speciesBounds?x1+padX:Math.min(W,x1+padX),ey=speciesBounds?y1+padY:Math.min(H,y1+padY);
    if(ex-sx<20){sx=Math.max(0,x0-90);ex=Math.min(W,x1+90);}if(ey-sy<20){sy=Math.max(0,y0-100);ey=Math.min(H,y1+100);}
    const sw=Math.max(1,ex-sx),sh=Math.max(1,ey-sy),cw=target.width,ch=target.height;
    /* zoom is output-canvas pixels per stage pixel; use the same transform for hit testing. */
    const zoom=Math.min((cw-22)/sw,(ch-22)/sh),dw=sw*zoom,dh=sh*zoom,dx=(cw-dw)/2,dy=(ch-dh)/2;
    const bg=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,0,0,ch);bg.addColorStop(0,'#1A1D29');bg.addColorStop(1,'#111218');
    ctx.setTransform(1,0,0,1,0,0);ctx.clearRect(0,0,cw,ch);ctx.fillStyle=bg;ctx.fillRect(0,0,cw,ch);
    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled=true;ctx.imageSmoothingQuality='high';
    try{
      ctx.save();ctx.translate(dx,dy);ctx.scale(zoom,zoom);ctx.translate(-sx,-sy);
      drawCharacterToContext(ctx,c);
      ctx.restore();
    }catch(err){try{ctx.restore();}catch(_e){}console.warn('Held-item preview render failed',err);}
    /* Character-sheet images can finish decoding after the first preview paint. Redraw
       this canvas on their load so the preview never stays on a soft/unfinished first frame. */
    const hookPreviewImages=cache=>{
      if(!cache||typeof cache!=='object')return;
      Object.keys(cache).forEach(key=>{
        const img=cache[key];
        if(img&&typeof img.addEventListener==='function'&&!img.complete&&!img.__evHeldPreviewSharpHook){
          img.__evHeldPreviewSharpHook=true;
          img.addEventListener('load',refreshHeldPreview,{once:true});
        }
      });
    };
    hookPreviewImages(ctx.__sheetImg);hookPreviewImages(ctx.__skinImgCache);

    /* Keep a generous hit target centred on the visible held object, not on the whole canvas. */
    const item=propState(c)[propSide];let objectHit=null;
    if(item&&item.src){
      const hs=geo.start('hand'+propSide),he=geo.end('hand'+propSide);
      if(hs&&he){
        const hx=hs.x+(he.x-hs.x)*.5,hy=hs.y+(he.y-hs.y)*.5;
        const angle=Math.atan2(he.y-hs.y,he.x-hs.x)-Math.PI/2+(Number(item.rotation)||0)*Math.PI/180;
        const ox=Number(item.offsetX)||0,oy=Number(item.offsetY)||0;
        const center=heldTransformPoint({x:hx+Math.cos(angle)*ox-Math.sin(angle)*oy,y:hy+Math.sin(angle)*ox+Math.cos(angle)*oy},c);
        const cx=dx+(center.x-sx)*zoom,cy=dy+(center.y-sy)*zoom;
        let aspect=1;try{const cached=window.__ethvelleHeldPropImageCache&&window.__ethvelleHeldPropImageCache.get(item.src);if(cached&&cached.naturalWidth&&cached.naturalHeight)aspect=cached.naturalWidth/cached.naturalHeight;}catch(_e){}
        const size=heldPropDimensions(c,item,aspect),imageH=size.h;
        const modelScale=Math.max(.05,Math.abs(Number(c.modelTransform&&c.modelTransform.scale)||1));
        const radius=Math.max(48,Math.min(110,Math.hypot(imageH*aspect,imageH)*.58*modelScale*zoom));
        objectHit={x:cx,y:cy,radius};
      }
    }
    heldPreviewView={zoom,sx,sy,sw,sh,dx,dy,dw,dh,charId:c.id,side:propSide,objectHit};
  }
  function refreshHeldPreview(){if(heldPreviewRaf)return;heldPreviewRaf=requestAnimationFrame(()=>{heldPreviewRaf=0;drawHeldCharacterPreview();});}
  function heldPropDimensions(char,item,aspect){
    const bodyKeys=['spine_lower','spine_upper','chest','neck','thighL','shinL','thighR','shinR'];
    const bodyLen=bodyKeys.reduce((sum,k)=>sum+Math.max(0,Number(char&&char.bones&&char.bones[k]&&char.bones[k].length)||0),0)||Number(typeof stageH!=='undefined'?stageH:420)||420;
    const scale=Math.max(.2,Math.min(2.5,Number(item&&item.scale)||1));
    const ratio=Number.isFinite(Number(aspect))&&Number(aspect)>0?Number(aspect):1;
    const rawH=Math.max(.5,bodyLen*.235*scale),rawW=Math.max(.5,rawH*ratio);
    /* Preserve aspect ratio and monotonic slider response; cap only the overall footprint. */
    const fit=Math.min(1,320/Math.max(rawH,rawW));
    return {w:Math.max(.5,rawW*fit),h:Math.max(.5,rawH*fit)};
  }
  function heldFlipLabel(item){
    if(!item||(!item.flipX&&!item.flipY))return 'Flip: Off';
    if(item.flipX&&item.flipY)return 'Flip: Both';
    return item.flipX?'Flip: Horizontal':'Flip: Vertical';
  }
  function heldRemoveButtonMarkup(){
    return '<button type="button" class="evpa-prop-remove-icon" aria-label="Remove held object" title="Remove object" onclick="removeCharacterHeldProp()"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.9" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M4 7h16M9 7V4h6v3m-9 0 1 13h10l1-13M10 11v5m4-5v5"/></svg></button>';
  }
  function syncHeldPropsSideUI(){
    const c=activeChar();if(!c)return;
    const back=heldBackdrop(),item=propState(c)[propSide];
    const sides=back.querySelectorAll('.evpa-action-side button');
    sides.forEach((button,index)=>{
      const side=index===0?'L':'R',active=propSideTouched&&propSide===side;
      button.classList.toggle('active',active);button.setAttribute('aria-pressed',active?'true':'false');
    });
    const preview=back.querySelector('#evpaPropPreview');
    if(preview)preview.innerHTML=item?`<img src="${safe(item.src)}" alt="Object image">${heldRemoveButtonMarkup()}`:'<span class="evpa-prop-empty">No object added</span>';
    const upload=back.querySelector('.evpa-prop-upload');
    if(upload)upload.textContent='＋ '+(item?'Replace object':'Add object image');
    const controls=back.querySelector('.evpa-prop-controls');
    if(controls){controls.style.opacity=item?'1':'.45';controls.style.pointerEvents=item?'auto':'none';}
    const values=[item?Number(item.scale||1)*100:100,item?Number(item.rotation)||0:0,item?Number(item.offsetX)||0:0,item?Number(item.offsetY)||0:0];
    const outs=['evpaPropScaleVal','evpaPropRotationVal','evpaPropXVal','evpaPropYVal'];
    const ranges=controls?controls.querySelectorAll('input[type=range]'):[];
    ranges.forEach((range,index)=>{if(index<values.length)range.value=String(values[index]);});
    outs.forEach((id,index)=>{const out=$(id);if(out)out.textContent=index===0?Math.round(values[index])+'%':index===1?Math.round(values[index])+'°':String(Math.round(values[index]));});
    const flip=back.querySelector('#evpaPropFlipButton');
    if(flip){flip.textContent=heldFlipLabel(item);flip.classList.toggle('active',!!(item&&(item.flipX||item.flipY)));flip.setAttribute('aria-pressed',item&&(item.flipX||item.flipY)?'true':'false');}
    refreshHeldPreview();
  }
  function renderHeldPropsPanel(){
    const c=activeChar();if(!c)return;const props=propState(c),item=props[propSide];
    const val=(v,d)=>Number.isFinite(Number(v))?Number(v):d;
    const scale=val(item&&item.scale,1),rotation=val(item&&item.rotation,0),x=val(item&&item.offsetX,0),y=val(item&&item.offsetY,0);
    const preview=item?`<img src="${safe(item.src)}" alt="Object image">${heldRemoveButtonMarkup()}`:`<span class="evpa-prop-empty">No object added</span>`;
    const back=heldBackdrop();
    back.innerHTML=`<div class="modal-sheet evpa-props-sheet" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="Character held object">
      <div class="sheet-handle"></div>
      <div class="evpa-action-side"><button type="button" class="${propSideTouched&&propSide==='L'?'active':''}" aria-pressed="${propSideTouched&&propSide==='L'}" onclick="setCharacterPropSide('L')">Left Hand</button><button type="button" class="${propSideTouched&&propSide==='R'?'active':''}" aria-pressed="${propSideTouched&&propSide==='R'}" onclick="setCharacterPropSide('R')">Right Hand</button></div>
      <div class="evpa-prop-preview" id="evpaPropPreview">${preview}</div>
      <input id="evpaPropFile" type="file" accept="image/png,image/webp,image/jpeg,image/*" hidden onchange="setCharacterHeldPropFile(this)">
      <button type="button" class="evpa-prop-upload" onclick="document.getElementById('evpaPropFile').click()">＋ ${item?'Replace object':'Add object image'}</button>
      <div class="evpa-prop-stage-wrap"><div class="evpa-prop-stage-title">Character Preview</div><canvas id="evpaPropStagePreview" width="1080" height="1512" aria-label="Full-body selected character with held object"></canvas><p class="evpa-prop-drag-hint">Drag the object in the preview to adjust its position</p></div>
      <div class="evpa-prop-controls" ${item?'':'style="opacity:.45;pointer-events:none"'}>
        <div class="evpa-prop-field"><label class="evpa-range"><span>Size <output id="evpaPropScaleVal">${Math.round(scale*100)}%</output></span><input type="range" min="20" max="250" step="1" value="${Math.round(scale*100)}" oninput="setCharacterHeldPropValue('scale',Number(this.value)/100)"></label></div>
        <div class="evpa-prop-field"><label class="evpa-range"><span>Rotate <output id="evpaPropRotationVal">${Math.round(rotation)}°</output></span><input type="range" min="-180" max="180" step="1" value="${Math.round(rotation)}" oninput="setCharacterHeldPropValue('rotation',Number(this.value))"></label></div>
        <div class="evpa-prop-field"><label class="evpa-range"><span>Move X <output id="evpaPropXVal">${Math.round(x)}</output></span><input type="range" id="evpaPropXInput" min="-100" max="100" step="1" value="${Math.round(x)}" oninput="setCharacterHeldPropValue('offsetX',Number(this.value))"></label></div>
        <div class="evpa-prop-field"><label class="evpa-range"><span>Move Y <output id="evpaPropYVal">${Math.round(y)}</output></span><input type="range" id="evpaPropYInput" min="-100" max="100" step="1" value="${Math.round(y)}" oninput="setCharacterHeldPropValue('offsetY',Number(this.value))"></label></div>
        <div class="evpa-prop-field evpa-prop-flip-field"><label class="evpa-range"><span>Flip</span></label><button type="button" id="evpaPropFlipButton" class="evpa-prop-flip-button ${item&&(item.flipX||item.flipY)?'active':''}" aria-pressed="${item&&(item.flipX||item.flipY)?'true':'false'}" onclick="toggleCharacterHeldProp('flip')">${heldFlipLabel(item)}</button></div>
      </div>
      <button type="button" class="evpa-close-btn" onclick="closeCharacterPropsPanel()">Done</button>
    </div>`;
    back.classList.add('active');
    const canvas=$('evpaPropStagePreview');
    if(canvas&&!canvas.dataset.dragReady){
      canvas.dataset.dragReady='1';
      canvas.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
        const cc=activeChar(),it=cc&&propState(cc)[propSide],view=heldPreviewView;
        if(!it||!view||view.charId!==cc.id||view.side!==propSide||!view.objectHit)return;
        const rect=canvas.getBoundingClientRect();if(!rect.width||!rect.height)return;
        const px=(e.clientX-rect.left)*(canvas.width/rect.width),py=(e.clientY-rect.top)*(canvas.height/rect.height);
        const hit=view.objectHit,dist=Math.hypot(px-hit.x,py-hit.y);
        /* Start a drag only on/very near the held object; tapping elsewhere will not move it. */
        if(dist>hit.radius+12)return;
        heldPreviewDrag={pointerId:e.pointerId,startX:e.clientX,startY:e.clientY,offsetX:Number(it.offsetX)||0,offsetY:Number(it.offsetY)||0,viewZoom:view.zoom,charId:cc.id,side:propSide};
        canvas.style.cursor='grabbing';try{canvas.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);}catch(_e){}e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
      });
      canvas.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{
        const d=heldPreviewDrag;if(!d||d.pointerId!==e.pointerId)return;
        const cc=activeChar(),it=cc&&propState(cc)[d.side];if(!cc||cc.id!==d.charId||!it)return;
        const rect=canvas.getBoundingClientRect();if(!rect.width||!rect.height)return;
        const dx=(e.clientX-d.startX)*(canvas.width/rect.width),dy=(e.clientY-d.startY)*(canvas.height/rect.height);
        const mt=cc.modelTransform||{},cr=-(Number(mt.rotation)||0)*Math.PI/180,cs=Math.cos(cr),sn=Math.sin(cr);
        const unzoomX=dx/Math.max(.0001,d.viewZoom),unzoomY=dy/Math.max(.0001,d.viewZoom);
        const rotatedX=unzoomX*cs-unzoomY*sn,rotatedY=unzoomX*sn+unzoomY*cs;
        const modelScale=Math.max(.05,Math.abs(Number(mt.scale)||1));
        const localX=rotatedX/(modelScale*(cc.mirrorX?-1:1)),localY=rotatedY/(modelScale*(cc.mirrorY?-1:1));
        const geo=heldBoneGeometry(cc),hand='hand'+d.side,hs=geo.start(hand),he=geo.end(hand);
        const handAngle=hs&&he?Math.atan2(he.y-hs.y,he.x-hs.x)-Math.PI/2+(Number(it.rotation)||0)*Math.PI/180:0;
        const hc=Math.cos(handAngle),hn=Math.sin(handAngle);
        it.offsetX=Math.max(-100,Math.min(100,d.offsetX+localX*hc+localY*hn));
        it.offsetY=Math.max(-100,Math.min(100,d.offsetY-localX*hn+localY*hc));
        const ox=$('evpaPropXVal'),oy=$('evpaPropYVal'),xi=$('evpaPropXInput'),yi=$('evpaPropYInput');if(ox)ox.textContent=Math.round(it.offsetX);if(oy)oy.textContent=Math.round(it.offsetY);if(xi)xi.value=Math.round(it.offsetX);if(yi)yi.value=Math.round(it.offsetY);
        if(typeof drawStick==='function')drawStick();refreshHeldPreview();e.preventDefault();
      });
      const finishHeldDrag=e=>{if(heldPreviewDrag&&(!e||heldPreviewDrag.pointerId===e.pointerId)){heldPreviewDrag=null;canvas.style.cursor='grab';if(e){try{canvas.releasePointerCapture(e.pointerId);}catch(_e){}}if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState();}};
      canvas.addEventListener('pointerup',finishHeldDrag);
      canvas.addEventListener('pointercancel',()=>finishHeldDrag(null));
      canvas.addEventListener('lostpointercapture',()=>finishHeldDrag(null));
    }
    refreshHeldPreview();
  }
  window.openCharacterPropsPanel=function(){const c=activeChar();if(!c){if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Select a character first',{icon:toastIcons.info});return;}propState(c);propSide='R';propSideTouched=false;renderHeldPropsPanel();};
  window.closeCharacterPropsPanel=function(){const el=$('ethvelleCharacterPropsBackdrop');if(el)el.classList.remove('active');heldPreviewDrag=null;};
  window.setCharacterPropSide=function(side){if(!['L','R'].includes(side)||side===propSide)return;propSide=side;propSideTouched=true;heldPreviewDrag=null;syncHeldPropsSideUI();};
  window.setCharacterHeldPropValue=function(field,value){
    const c=activeChar(),item=c&&propState(c)[propSide];if(!item||!['scale','rotation','offsetX','offsetY'].includes(field)||!Number.isFinite(Number(value)))return;
    item[field]=Number(value);
    const ids={scale:'evpaPropScaleVal',rotation:'evpaPropRotationVal',offsetX:'evpaPropXVal',offsetY:'evpaPropYVal'},out=$(ids[field]);
    if(out)out.textContent=field==='scale'?Math.round(item.scale*100)+'%':field==='rotation'?Math.round(item.rotation)+'°':Math.round(item[field]);
    if(typeof drawStick==='function')drawStick();if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState();refreshHeldPreview();
  };
  window.toggleCharacterHeldProp=function(field){if(field!=='flip')return;const c=activeChar(),item=c&&propState(c)[propSide];if(!item)return;const state=item.flipX?(item.flipY?3:1):(item.flipY?2:0),next=(state+1)%4;item.flipX=next===1||next===3;item.flipY=next===2||next===3;if(typeof drawStick==='function')drawStick();if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState();const button=$('evpaPropFlipButton');if(button){button.textContent=heldFlipLabel(item);button.classList.toggle('active',next!==0);button.setAttribute('aria-pressed',next===0?'false':'true');}refreshHeldPreview();};
  window.removeCharacterHeldProp=function(){const c=activeChar();if(!c)return;propState(c)[propSide]=null;if(typeof drawStick==='function')drawStick();if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState();renderHeldPropsPanel();};
  window.setCharacterHeldPropFile=function(input){
    const file=input&&input.files&&input.files[0];if(input)input.value='';if(!file)return;const c=activeChar();if(!c)return;
    const reader=new FileReader();reader.onerror=()=>{if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Could not read the object image',{icon:toastIcons.info});};
    reader.onload=()=>{const img=new Image();img.onerror=()=>{if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Could not decode the object image',{icon:toastIcons.info});};img.onload=()=>{
      const max=640,ratio=Math.min(1,max/Math.max(img.naturalWidth,img.naturalHeight)),w=Math.max(1,Math.round(img.naturalWidth*ratio)),h=Math.max(1,Math.round(img.naturalHeight*ratio));
      const cv=document.createElement('canvas');cv.width=w;cv.height=h;const cx=cv.getContext('2d');if(!cx){if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Object image could not be prepared',{icon:toastIcons.info});return;}cx.clearRect(0,0,w,h);cx.drawImage(img,0,0,w,h);
      propSide='R';propSideTouched=true;propState(c)[propSide]={src:cv.toDataURL('image/png'),name:file.name||'Object',scale:1,rotation:0,offsetX:0,offsetY:0,flipX:false,flipY:false};
      const clip=typeof selectedClip!=='undefined'&&typeof getClipByRef==='function'?getClipByRef(selectedClip):null,parts=c.sheet&&c.sheet.parts||{},field=propSide==='L'?'leftHand':'rightHand';
      if(clip&&parts['hand'+propSide+'_hold']){const a=typeof normalizeActionsV128==='function'?normalizeActionsV128(clip.faceActions):{};clip.faceActions={...a,[field]:'hold'};}
      if(typeof drawStick==='function')drawStick();if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState();renderHeldPropsPanel();
      if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Object attached at the '+(propSide==='L'?'left':'right')+' hand',{icon:toastIcons.check,duration:1400});
    };img.src=String(reader.result||'');};reader.readAsDataURL(file);
  };
  // Render the object at the centre of the selected hand bone, keeping the same transform in previews and exports.
  window.ethvelleDrawCharacterProps=function(targetCtx,char,boneStart,boneEnd){
    const props=char&&char.handProps;if(!props)return;
    const cache=window.__ethvelleHeldPropImageCache||(window.__ethvelleHeldPropImageCache=new Map());
    ['L','R'].forEach(side=>{
      const item=props[side];if(!item||!item.src)return;let img=cache.get(item.src);
      if(!img){img=new Image();img.onload=()=>{if(typeof drawStick==='function')drawStick();if($('evpaPropStagePreview'))refreshHeldPreview();};img.onerror=()=>{};cache.set(item.src,img);img.src=item.src;}
      if(!img.complete||!img.naturalWidth)return;
      let start,end;try{start=boneStart('hand'+side);end=boneEnd('hand'+side);}catch(_e){return;}
      if(!start||!end||![start.x,start.y,end.x,end.y].every(Number.isFinite))return;
      const dx=end.x-start.x,dy=end.y-start.y,angle=Math.atan2(dy,dx)-Math.PI/2+(Number(item.rotation)||0)*Math.PI/180;
      const handX=start.x+dx*.5,handY=start.y+dy*.5;
      const aspect=img.naturalWidth/Math.max(1,img.naturalHeight),size=heldPropDimensions(char,item,aspect),w=size.w,h=size.h;
      targetCtx.save();targetCtx.translate(handX,handY);targetCtx.rotate(angle);targetCtx.translate(Number(item.offsetX)||0,Number(item.offsetY)||0);targetCtx.scale(item.flipX?-1:1,item.flipY?-1:1);targetCtx.drawImage(img,-w*.5,-h*.5,w,h);targetCtx.restore();
    });
  };

  /* Searchable Animate panel. Preserve the existing preview/apply/paid-plan behavior. */
  let animationSearchQuery='';
  let animationCategory='';
  function activeAnimationModelType(){
    let c=null;try{c=typeof activeCharacter==='function'?activeCharacter():null;}catch(_e){}
    const type=String(c&&(c.modelType||(c.sheet&&c.sheet.modelType))||'humanoid');
    if(type==='animal'||type==='bird'||type==='mermaid'||type==='lamia')return type;
    if(type==='ghost'||type==='fairy'||type==='devil')return 'humanoid'; // legacy rigs: no dedicated category remains
    return (c&&((c.sheet&&c.sheet.beast)||c.beast))?'beastman':'humanoid';
  }
  window.setCharacterAnimationCategory=function(type){

  };

  window.filterCharacterAnimations=function(value){animationSearchQuery=String(value||'').trim().toLowerCase();const bar=$('evcaAnimSearchBar');if(bar)bar.classList.toggle('has-query',!!animationSearchQuery);window.renderAnimList();};
  window.clearCharacterAnimationSearch=function(){animationSearchQuery='';const input=$('evcaAnimSearchInput');if(input)input.value='';const bar=$('evcaAnimSearchBar');if(bar)bar.classList.remove('has-query');window.renderAnimList();if(input)input.focus();};
  window.renderAnimList=function(){
    const container=$('animListBody');if(!container)return;
