      const ctx=audioOverlayAudioCtx;
      if(ctx&&isPlaying&&ctx.state==='suspended'){
        try{const p=ctx.resume();if(p&&typeof p.catch==='function')p.catch(err=>console.warn('Ethvelle audio context stayed suspended:',err&&err.name||'Error',err));}catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle audio context resume:',err);}
      }
      // The existing v65/v66/v67 graph builders all terminate nodes.gain at destination.
      // Mark/repair only a plain graph that none of those wrappers has claimed; do not
      // reconnect processed gain nodes (which would duplicate their output).
      (Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:[]).filter(o=>o&&o.type==='audio').forEach(ov=>{
        const nodes=audioOverlayNodes&&audioOverlayNodes[String(ov.id)];if(!nodes||!nodes.gain||!ctx)return;
        if(!nodes.__evcaDsp&&!nodes.__evcaV66&&!nodes.__evcaV67&&!nodes.__evV140DirectOutput){
          try{nodes.gain.connect(ctx.destination);}catch(_e){}
          nodes.__evV140DirectOutput=true;
        }
      });
      return result;
    };
    wrappedAudioRender.__ethvelleV140Context=true;window.renderAudioOverlayMedia=wrappedAudioRender;
  }

  if(typeof updateHistoryButtons==='function')updateHistoryButtons();
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v141-smooth-video-slide-history-fix">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  if(window.__ethvelleV141SmoothSlideFixes)return;
  window.__ethvelleV141SmoothSlideFixes=true;

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------------
     BUG 1  -  Keep a gesture-primed future video parked on its exact first
     source frame. v140 calls play() on every clip inside the Play gesture,
     then pauses future clips. That short priming playback advances currentTime;
     the boundary gate later requires <=40ms alignment and otherwise stalls the
     whole timeline while a new seek/decode completes. Reset the parked warm
     element immediately after that priming pause, and re-check against the live
     clip at adoption (trim/split/undo may have changed its source offset).
     ------------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  function v141LiveOverlay(id){
    return Array.isArray(overlays)
      ? overlays.find(o=>o&&String(o.id)===String(id))||null
      : null;
  }
  const priorSetupVideo=window.setupOverlayVideoEl;
  if(typeof priorSetupVideo==='function'&&!priorSetupVideo.__ethvelleV141WarmReset){
    const wrappedSetupVideo=function(el,ov){
      const result=priorSetupVideo.apply(this,arguments);
      if(el&&el.tagName==='VIDEO'&&!el.__evV141WarmResetListener){
        const resetGesturePrimedPosition=()=>{
          if(el.dataset.evWarmElement!=='1'||el.dataset.evWarmRole!=='v140-gesture-prime')return;
          const live=v141LiveOverlay(el.dataset.evV72OverlayId|| (ov&&ov.id));
          if(!live||live.type!=='video'||live.reversePlayback)return;
          const target=Math.max(0,Number(live.sourceOffset)||0);
          el.dataset.evWarmTarget=String(target);
          if(el.readyState>=1&&!el.seeking&&Math.abs((Number(el.currentTime)||0)-target)>0.015){
            const now=performance.now();
            el.dataset.evSeekRequestAt=String(now);
            try{el.currentTime=target;}catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle warm-video source reset:',err&&err.name||'Error',err);}
          }
        };
        // The v140 prime promise pauses future clips after a short user-gesture play.
        // Rewind on that pause so the decoder is already parked at sourceOffset before
        // the timeline reaches the next clip. This does not touch an adopted/live clip.
        el.addEventListener('pause',resetGesturePrimedPosition);
        el.__evV141WarmResetListener=true;
      }
      return result;
    };
    wrappedSetupVideo.__ethvelleV141WarmReset=true;
    window.setupOverlayVideoEl=wrappedSetupVideo;
  }

  const priorReleaseVideo=window.releaseOverlayVideoEl;
  if(typeof priorReleaseVideo==='function'&&!priorReleaseVideo.__ethvelleV141LoopReset){
    const wrappedReleaseVideo=function(el){
      const result=priorReleaseVideo.apply(this,arguments);
      // v140 parks finished clip decoders for the next project loop. They must be
      // rewound after parking; otherwise the next loop inherits the previous end frame
      // and the same boundary gate waits for a full-duration corrective seek.
      if(el&&el.tagName==='VIDEO'&&el.dataset.evWarmElement==='1'&&el.dataset.evWarmRole==='v140-loop-pool'){
        const live=v141LiveOverlay(el.dataset.evV72OverlayId||'');
        if(live&&live.type==='video'&&!live.reversePlayback){
          const target=Math.max(0,Number(live.sourceOffset)||0);
          el.dataset.evWarmTarget=String(target);
          if(el.readyState>=1&&!el.seeking&&Math.abs((Number(el.currentTime)||0)-target)>0.015){
            const now=performance.now();el.dataset.evSeekRequestAt=String(now);
            try{el.currentTime=target;}catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle loop-video source reset:',err&&err.name||'Error',err);}
          }
        }
      }
      return result;
    };
    wrappedReleaseVideo.__ethvelleV141LoopReset=true;
    window.releaseOverlayVideoEl=wrappedReleaseVideo;
  }

  const priorTakeWarm=window.takeWarmOverlayVideo;
  if(typeof priorTakeWarm==='function'&&!priorTakeWarm.__ethvelleV141BoundarySync){
    const wrappedTakeWarm=function(ov){
      const el=priorTakeWarm.apply(this,arguments);
      if(el&&ov&&el.tagName==='VIDEO'&&el.paused&&el.readyState>=1&&!el.seeking){
        const t=Math.max(Number(currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0,Number(ov.trimStart)||0);
        let expected=Number(ov.sourceOffset)||0;
        try{if(typeof evOverlayVideoExpectedLocalTime==='function')expected=evOverlayVideoExpectedLocalTime(ov,t,el);}catch(_e){}
        if(Number.isFinite(expected)&&Math.abs((Number(el.currentTime)||0)-expected)>0.035){
          const now=performance.now();
          el.dataset.evSeekRequestAt=String(now);
          el.dataset.evPlaySeekAt=String(now);
          try{el.currentTime=expected;}catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle incoming-video alignment:',err&&err.name||'Error',err);}
        }
      }
      return el;
    };
    wrappedTakeWarm.__ethvelleV141BoundarySync=true;
    window.takeWarmOverlayVideo=wrappedTakeWarm;
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------------
     BUG 2  -  Exclude ONLY the four directional In/Out slide presets from the
     history signature and payload. They stay in the live project and saved
     project, but Undo/Redo never changes them. Other animation presets,
     durations, filters and real edits remain normal history entries.
     ------------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  const DIRECTIONAL_SLIDES=new Set(['slide-left','slide-right','slide-up','slide-down']);
  const HISTORY_PREFIX='__ethvelle_v140__';
  const historyPayloads=window.__ethvelleV140HistoryPayloads;
  const slideState=window.__ethvelleV141SlideHistoryState||
    (window.__ethvelleV141SlideHistoryState=new Map());

  function slideKey(ov){return String(ov&&ov.id!=null?ov.id:'');}
  function captureCurrentSlidePresets(){
    if(!Array.isArray(overlays))return;
    overlays.forEach(ov=>{
      if(!ov||ov.id==null)return;
      const key=slideKey(ov),cfg=ov.clipAnimations&&typeof ov.clipAnimations==='object'?ov.clipAnimations:{};
      const entry=slideState.get(key)||{};
      ['in','out'].forEach(slot=>{
        const value=cfg[slot];
        if(DIRECTIONAL_SLIDES.has(String(value)))entry[slot]=String(value);
        else delete entry[slot];
      });
      if(entry.in||entry.out)slideState.set(key,entry);
      else slideState.delete(key);
    });
    while(slideState.size>512)slideState.delete(slideState.keys().next().value);
  }
  function stripDirectionalSlides(value,parentKey){
    if(Array.isArray(value))return value.map(item=>stripDirectionalSlides(item,''));
    if(!value||typeof value!=='object')return value;
    const out={};
    Object.keys(value).sort().forEach(key=>{
      if(parentKey==='clipAnimations'&&(key==='in'||key==='out')&&DIRECTIONAL_SLIDES.has(String(value[key])))return;
      const child=stripDirectionalSlides(value[key],key);
      if(child!==undefined)out[key]=child;
    });
    if(parentKey==='clipAnimations'){
      const hasActive=['in','out','overall'].some(key=>out[key]&&out[key]!=='none');
      if(!hasActive){
        // getAnimationConfig() materializes these defaults when the first preset is
        // selected. Remove that inert scaffolding too, so a slide-only choice has the
        // exact same history signature as the untouched clip and cannot cut Redo.
        if(out.in==='none')delete out.in;
        if(out.out==='none')delete out.out;
        if(out.overall==='none')delete out.overall;
        if(Number(out.inDuration)===0.45)delete out.inDuration;
        if(Number(out.outDuration)===0.45)delete out.outDuration;
        if(Number(out.overallDuration)===1.2)delete out.overallDuration;
      }
      if(!Object.keys(out).length)return undefined;
    }
    return out;
  }
  function normalizeHistorySignature(value,inCharacters,parentKey){
    if(Array.isArray(value))return value.map(item=>normalizeHistorySignature(item,inCharacters,''));
    if(!value||typeof value!=='object')return value;
    const out={};
    Object.keys(value).sort().forEach(key=>{
      if(key.startsWith('__')||key==='peaks'||key==='thumbs')return;
      if(['currentAbsoluteTimeSec','playheadTimeSec','playhead','currentTime','frame','currentFrame','runtimeFrame'].includes(key))return;
      if(inCharacters&&['bones','baseAngles','modelTransform','restTransform','hipRestY'].includes(key))return;
      if(parentKey==='clipAnimations'&&(key==='in'||key==='out')&&DIRECTIONAL_SLIDES.has(String(value[key])))return;
      out[key]=normalizeHistorySignature(value[key],inCharacters||key==='characters',key);
    });
    return out;
  }
  function stableHistoryString(value){
    if(Array.isArray(value))return '['+value.map(stableHistoryString).join(',')+']';
    if(value&&typeof value==='object')return '{'+Object.keys(value).sort().map(key=>JSON.stringify(key)+':'+stableHistoryString(value[key])).join(',')+'}';
    return JSON.stringify(value);
  }

  const priorSnapshotState=window.snapshotState;
  if(typeof priorSnapshotState==='function'&&!priorSnapshotState.__ethvelleV141SlideHistory){
    const wrappedSnapshotState=function(){
      captureCurrentSlidePresets();
      const baseSignature=priorSnapshotState.apply(this,arguments);
      if(!historyPayloads||typeof baseSignature!=='string')return baseSignature;
      try{
        const payloadText=historyPayloads.get(baseSignature);
        if(!payloadText)return baseSignature;
        const payload=stripDirectionalSlides(JSON.parse(payloadText),'' );
        const normalized=normalizeHistorySignature(payload,false,'');
        const signature=HISTORY_PREFIX+stableHistoryString(normalized);
        // Store a compact, direction-free payload under the returned signature.
        historyPayloads.set(signature,JSON.stringify(payload));
        return signature;
      }catch(err){
        console.warn('Ethvelle directional-slide history normalization:',err);
        return baseSignature;
      }
    };
    wrappedSnapshotState.__ethvelleV141SlideHistory=true;
    window.snapshotState=wrappedSnapshotState;
  }

  function mergeCurrentDirectionalSlides(restoredData){
    if(!restoredData||!Array.isArray(restoredData.overlays))return;
    const currentById=new Map((Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:[]).filter(Boolean).map(ov=>[slideKey(ov),ov]));
    restoredData.overlays.forEach(target=>{
      if(!target||target.id==null)return;
      const key=slideKey(target),current=currentById.get(key)||null;
      const cached=slideState.get(key)||{};
      const targetCfg=target.clipAnimations&&typeof target.clipAnimations==='object'?target.clipAnimations:(target.clipAnimations={});
      const liveCfg=current&&current.clipAnimations&&typeof current.clipAnimations==='object'?current.clipAnimations:{};
      ['in','out'].forEach(slot=>{
        const liveValue=liveCfg[slot];
        const cachedValue=cached[slot];
        const savedValue=targetCfg[slot];
        let preserve;
        if(DIRECTIONAL_SLIDES.has(String(liveValue)))preserve=String(liveValue);
        else if(DIRECTIONAL_SLIDES.has(String(cachedValue)))preserve=String(cachedValue);
        else if(DIRECTIONAL_SLIDES.has(String(savedValue))){
          // A legacy/older snapshot may still contain a directional slide. Keep the
          // current non-slide choice (Fade/None/Zoom/etc), or remove the slide if none
          // is currently selected; do not let Undo/Redo introduce it.
          if(liveValue!==undefined&&liveValue!==null)preserve=liveValue;
        }
        if(preserve!==undefined)targetCfg[slot]=preserve;
        else if(DIRECTIONAL_SLIDES.has(String(savedValue)))delete targetCfg[slot];
      });
      if(!Object.keys(targetCfg).length)delete target.clipAnimations;
    });
  }

  const priorApplySnapshot=window.applySnapshot;
  if(typeof priorApplySnapshot==='function'&&!priorApplySnapshot.__ethvelleV141SlideHistory){
    const wrappedApplySnapshot=function(snapshot){
      captureCurrentSlidePresets();
      if(historyPayloads&&typeof snapshot==='string'){
        try{
          let payloadText=historyPayloads.get(snapshot)||null;
          if(!payloadText&&snapshot.startsWith(HISTORY_PREFIX))payloadText=snapshot.slice(HISTORY_PREFIX.length);
          let data=payloadText?JSON.parse(payloadText):JSON.parse(snapshot);
          if(data&&typeof data==='object'&&Array.isArray(data.overlays)){
            mergeCurrentDirectionalSlides(data);
            // The previous v140 restore wrapper still performs waveform-cache rehydration,
            // UI rebuild and its settled history-head refresh when given ordinary JSON.
            return priorApplySnapshot.call(this,JSON.stringify(data));
          }
        }catch(err){
          console.warn('Ethvelle directional-slide restore merge:',err);
        }
      }
      return priorApplySnapshot.apply(this,arguments);
    };
    wrappedApplySnapshot.__ethvelleV141SlideHistory=true;
    window.applySnapshot=wrappedApplySnapshot;
  }

  if(typeof updateHistoryButtons==='function')updateHistoryButtons();
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v142-smooth-transition-character-ui-fix">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  if(window.__ethvelleV142SmoothTransitionCharacterUI)return;
  window.__ethvelleV142SmoothTransitionCharacterUI=true;

  /* ----------------------------------------------------------------------
     1) Smooth video cuts.
     v141 rewound each gesture-primed incoming decoder in its pause listener.
     That discarded the decoded first frame and forced Android WebView to seek
     and decode it again at the clip boundary. Keep the already-decoded parked
     frame instead. While the element is paused and still carries this marker,
     treat its parked currentTime as its prepared start frame. This skips only
     the redundant boundary seek; once the incoming video actually starts,
     normal timeline-time sync/rate correction resumes immediately.
     ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */
  const V142_WARM_ROLE='v142-gesture-prime';

  function markGesturePrimedVideo(el){
    if(!el||el.tagName!=='VIDEO'||el.dataset.evWarmElement!=='1')return;
    el.dataset.evWarmRole=V142_WARM_ROLE; // v141's pause handler only rewinds the old role.
    el.dataset.evV142GestureFrame='1';
  }
  const priorSetupVideo=window.setupOverlayVideoEl;
  if(typeof priorSetupVideo==='function'&&!priorSetupVideo.__ethvelleV142KeepDecodedFrame){
    const wrappedSetupVideo=function(el,ov){
      const result=priorSetupVideo.apply(this,arguments);
      if(el&&el.tagName==='VIDEO'&&el.dataset.evWarmRole==='v140-gesture-prime')markGesturePrimedVideo(el);
      return result;
    };
    wrappedSetupVideo.__ethvelleV142KeepDecodedFrame=true;
    window.setupOverlayVideoEl=wrappedSetupVideo;
  }

  const priorExpected=window.evOverlayVideoExpectedLocalTime;
  if(typeof priorExpected==='function'&&!priorExpected.__ethvelleV142ParkedFrame){
    const wrappedExpected=function(ov,timeSec,el){
      const real=priorExpected.apply(this,arguments);
      // BUGFIX v150: this used to trust el.currentTime unconditionally for ANY paused
      // gesture-primed element, so a clip whose priming play() ran a little long before
      // being paused again reported itself "on time" at the WRONG source frame  -  it then
      // opened on that wrong frame and had to be dragged back into sync by the rate servo
      // after it went live (visible as a jump/offset start on every clip). Only skip the
      // redundant seek when the parked frame is already within one frame of the real
      // expected position; otherwise defer to the real value so the normal alignment/seek
      // path corrects it before the clip is ever shown.
      if(el&&el.tagName==='VIDEO'&&el.dataset.evV142GestureFrame==='1'&&
         el.paused&&!el.seeking&&Number.isFinite(Number(el.currentTime))&&Number.isFinite(real)&&
         Math.abs((Number(el.currentTime)||0)-real)<=(1/Math.max(1,Number(FPS)||30))){
        // The priming play has already decoded this frame, and it's genuinely where the
        // timeline expects it. Do not ask the boundary gate or v141's adoption wrapper to
        // re-seek back to the same source start.
        if(window.__evVideoDebug)try{console.log('[evVideo] keep-parked-frame',el.dataset.evV72OverlayId,'parked',el.currentTime,'expected',real);}catch(_e){}
        return Math.max(0,Number(el.currentTime)||0);
      }
      if(window.__evVideoDebug&&el&&el.dataset&&el.dataset.evV142GestureFrame==='1'&&el.paused&&!el.seeking&&Number.isFinite(real))try{console.log('[evVideo] discard-parked-frame (drifted)',el.dataset.evV72OverlayId,'parked',el.currentTime,'expected',real);}catch(_e){}
      return real;
    };
    wrappedExpected.__ethvelleV142ParkedFrame=true;
    window.evOverlayVideoExpectedLocalTime=wrappedExpected;
  }

  const priorTakeWarm=window.takeWarmOverlayVideo;
  if(typeof priorTakeWarm==='function'&&!priorTakeWarm.__ethvelleV142AdoptFrame){
    const wrappedTakeWarm=function(ov){
      const id=String(ov&&ov.id!=null?ov.id:'');
      const candidate=typeof overlayWarmEls!=='undefined'&&overlayWarmEls?overlayWarmEls[id]:null;
      const wasGesturePrimed=!!(candidate&&candidate.dataset.evV142GestureFrame==='1');
      const el=priorTakeWarm.apply(this,arguments);
      if(el&&wasGesturePrimed){
        // Keep the marker through adoption. The actual 'playing' event clears it;
        // that lets the parked frame pass readiness checks without masking later sync.
        el.dataset.evV142GestureFrame='1';
        if(!el.__evV142ClearPrimeOnLivePlay){
          el.addEventListener('playing',function(){
            if(el.dataset.evWarmElement!=='1')delete el.dataset.evV142GestureFrame;
          });
          el.__evV142ClearPrimeOnLivePlay=true;
        }
      }
      return el;
    };
    wrappedTakeWarm.__ethvelleV142AdoptFrame=true;
    window.takeWarmOverlayVideo=wrappedTakeWarm;
  }

  // The v140 wrapper primes every video synchronously inside the trusted Play gesture.
  // Existing warm elements (created before Play) also need the v142 role before their
  // asynchronous play() promise pauses them, so v141 cannot rewind them on that pause.
  const priorStartPlayback=window.startPlayback;
  if(typeof priorStartPlayback==='function'&&!priorStartPlayback.__ethvelleV142KeepPrimed){
    const wrappedStartPlayback=function(){
      const markExistingWarmVideos=()=>{
        if(typeof overlayWarmEls!=='undefined'&&overlayWarmEls){
          Object.keys(overlayWarmEls).forEach(id=>markGesturePrimedVideo(overlayWarmEls[id]));
        }
      };
      // Mark already-created warm elements before v140 calls play(). This also covers
      // legacy WebViews whose play() returns no Promise and whose fallback pauses inline.
      markExistingWarmVideos();
      const result=priorStartPlayback.apply(this,arguments);
      if(typeof isPlaying!=='undefined'&&isPlaying)markExistingWarmVideos();
      return result;
    };
    wrappedStartPlayback.__ethvelleV142KeepPrimed=true;
    window.startPlayback=wrappedStartPlayback;
  }

  /* ----------------------------------------------------------------------
     2) Character held-object preview: retain the full-body crop and 1080px-wide
     backing bitmap across panel re-renders, and prevent touch drags on the preview
     or its sliders from scrolling the sheet. The actual hand/object drag logic is
     unchanged and continues to use the original pointer handlers.
     ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */
  const propsBackdrop=document.getElementById('ethvelleCharacterPropsBackdrop');
  let lastPreviewCanvas=null;
  function rangeProgress(input){
    if(!input||input.type!=='range')return;
    const min=Number(input.min||0),max=Number(input.max||100),value=Number(input.value||0);
    const percent=max>min?Math.max(0,Math.min(100,(value-min)/(max-min)*100)):0;
    input.style.setProperty('--ev-range-progress',percent+'%');
  }
  function tuneHeldPropsPanel(){
    const back=document.getElementById('ethvelleCharacterPropsBackdrop');
    if(!back||!back.classList.contains('active'))return;
    const canvas=back.querySelector('#evpaPropStagePreview');
    if(canvas&&canvas!==lastPreviewCanvas){
      lastPreviewCanvas=canvas;
      if(canvas.width!==1080)canvas.width=1080;
      if(canvas.height!==1512)canvas.height=1512;
      canvas.style.touchAction='pan-y';
      canvas.style.userSelect='none';
      canvas.style.webkitUserSelect='none';
      // Do not cancel touch on the whole preview. The sheet must remain vertically
      // scrollable when a finger starts on the character; the existing object-hit
      // pointer handler below only captures gestures that begin on the held object.
      canvas.__evV142NoSheetScroll=true;
    }
    back.querySelectorAll('.evpa-props-sheet .evpa-prop-field input[type="range"]').forEach(rangeProgress);
  }
  if(typeof MutationObserver==='function'&&document.body){
    const observer=new MutationObserver(()=>{
      const canvas=document.querySelector('#ethvelleCharacterPropsBackdrop #evpaPropStagePreview');
      if(canvas!==lastPreviewCanvas)tuneHeldPropsPanel();
    });
    observer.observe(document.body,{subtree:true,childList:true});
  }
  document.addEventListener('input',function(e){
    if(e.target&&e.target.matches&&e.target.matches('#ethvelleCharacterPropsBackdrop .evpa-props-sheet input[type="range"]'))rangeProgress(e.target);
  },true);
  ['openCharacterPropsPanel','setCharacterPropSide','toggleCharacterHeldProp','removeCharacterHeldProp'].forEach(name=>{
    const prior=window[name];
    if(typeof prior!=='function'||prior.__ethvelleV142PanelTune)return;
    const wrapped=function(){const result=prior.apply(this,arguments);tuneHeldPropsPanel();return result;};
    wrapped.__ethvelleV142PanelTune=true;window[name]=wrapped;
  });
  // FileReader completion rebuilds the panel asynchronously, so MutationObserver above
  // also catches that canvas replacement and restores its high-resolution backing store.

  /* ----------------------------------------------------------------------
     3) Put each custom-part '+' action inside the choice grid as its final tile.
     Existing model editor navigation and upload handling are reused unchanged.
     ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */
  function placePartAddTiles(){
    const back=document.getElementById('ethvelleClipFaceActionsBackdrop');
    if(!back||!back.classList.contains('active'))return;
    back.querySelectorAll('.evpa-section').forEach(section=>{
      const headingRow=section.querySelector(':scope > .evpa-section-heading');
      let heading=section.querySelector(':scope > .evpa-section-title');
      let oldAdd=null;
      if(headingRow){
        if(!heading)heading=headingRow.querySelector('.evpa-section-title');
        oldAdd=headingRow.querySelector('.evpa-section-add');
        if(oldAdd)oldAdd.remove();
        if(heading)section.insertBefore(heading,headingRow);
        headingRow.remove();
      }
      if(!heading)return;
      const label=String(heading.textContent||'').trim();
      if(/^blink\s+mode$/i.test(label))return;
      const grid=section.querySelector(':scope > .evpa-choice-grid');
      if(!grid||grid.querySelector(':scope > .evpa-choice-add'))return;
      const add=document.createElement('button');
      add.type='button';add.className='evpa-choice evpa-choice-add';
      add.title='Add custom '+label.toLowerCase()+' artwork';
      add.setAttribute('aria-label','Add custom '+label.toLowerCase()+' artwork');
      add.innerHTML='<span class="evpa-choice-preview"><span class="evpa-choice-add-icon" aria-hidden="true"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.6" stroke-linecap="round"><path d="M12 5v14M5 12h14"/></svg></span></span><span class="evpa-choice-label">Add part</span>';
      add.addEventListener('click',function(e){
        e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
        if(typeof window.openCustomPartEditorFromAction==='function')window.openCustomPartEditorFromAction();
        else if(typeof window.openSelectedCharacterModelEditor==='function'){window.closeClipCharacterActions();window.openSelectedCharacterModelEditor();}
      });
      grid.appendChild(add);
    });
  }
  const priorOpenCharacterActions=window.openClipCharacterActions;
  if(typeof priorOpenCharacterActions==='function'&&!priorOpenCharacterActions.__ethvelleV142GridAddTiles){
    const wrappedOpenCharacterActions=function(){
      const result=priorOpenCharacterActions.apply(this,arguments);
      placePartAddTiles();
      requestAnimationFrame(placePartAddTiles);
      return result;
    };
    wrappedOpenCharacterActions.__ethvelleV142GridAddTiles=true;
    window.openClipCharacterActions=wrappedOpenCharacterActions;
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',tuneHeldPropsPanel,{once:true});
  else tuneHeldPropsPanel();
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v146-held-preview-full-scroll">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  if(window.__ethvelleV146HeldPreviewFullScrollInstalled)return;
  window.__ethvelleV146HeldPreviewFullScrollInstalled=true;

  let gesture=null;
  const ROOT='#ethvelleCharacterPropsBackdrop';
  const STAGE='.evpa-prop-stage-wrap';

  function getPreviewSheet(e){
    const back=document.querySelector(ROOT+'.active');
    if(!back)return null;
    const stage=back.querySelector(STAGE);
    const sheet=back.querySelector('.evpa-props-sheet');
    if(!stage||!sheet||!(e.target===stage||stage.contains(e.target)))return null;
    return {stage,sheet};
  }

  // Intercept the preview gesture before the canvas's held-object drag listener.
  // A vertical gesture scrolls the complete sheet; a horizontal gesture is left
  // to the existing object-positioning handler.
  document.addEventListener('pointerdown',function(e){
    if(e.pointerType==='mouse'&&e.button!==0)return;
    const target=getPreviewSheet(e);
    if(!target)return;
    gesture={pointerId:e.pointerId,startX:e.clientX,startY:e.clientY,lastX:e.clientX,
      lastY:e.clientY,mode:'pending',sheet:target.sheet};
  },{capture:true,passive:true});

  document.addEventListener('pointermove',function(e){
    const g=gesture;
    if(!g||g.pointerId!==e.pointerId)return;
    const totalX=e.clientX-g.startX;
    const totalY=e.clientY-(g.startY===undefined?e.clientY:g.startY);
    if(g.mode==='pending'&&Math.max(Math.abs(totalX),Math.abs(totalY))>6){
      if(Math.abs(totalY)>=Math.abs(totalX)*0.9)g.mode='scroll';
      else if(Math.abs(totalX)>Math.abs(totalY)*0.9)g.mode='object';
    }
    if(g.mode==='scroll'){
      const max=Math.max(0,g.sheet.scrollHeight-g.sheet.clientHeight);
      if(max>0){
        // Incremental scrolling avoids the short travel/clamping seen with the old
        // fixed startScroll calculation and continues correctly across long swipes.
        const delta=g.lastY-e.clientY;
        g.sheet.scrollTop=Math.max(0,Math.min(max,g.sheet.scrollTop+delta));
        if(e.cancelable)e.preventDefault();
        e.stopPropagation();
        if(e.stopImmediatePropagation)e.stopImmediatePropagation();
      }
    }
    g.lastX=e.clientX;
    g.lastY=e.clientY;
  },{capture:true,passive:false});

  function finish(e){
    if(gesture&&(!e||gesture.pointerId===e.pointerId))gesture=null;
  }
  document.addEventListener('pointerup',finish,true);
  document.addEventListener('pointercancel',finish,true);
  document.addEventListener('lostpointercapture',finish,true);
})();
</script>

<!-- Ethvelle v148: mirrored held object, reordered controls, right-hand default,
     single Flip control, and custom-part plus icon border cleanup. -->
<style id="ethvelle-v148-held-items-and-custom-part-css">
  /* Use the newer single four-state Flip control. Hide only the legacy pair of
     separate Flip Horizontal / Flip Vertical buttons from the older panel. */
  #screen-editor .evpa-props-sheet .evpa-prop-toggle-row{display:none!important;}
  #screen-editor .evpa-props-sheet .evpa-prop-flip-field{display:block!important;}

  /* Remove the pale blue/white rim on the blue custom-part plus tile itself.
     The white plus glyph remains fully visible; the parent part tile is unchanged. */
  html body #screen-editor .evca-char-actions-sheet .evpa-choice.evpa-choice-add .evpa-choice-preview .evpa-choice-add-icon,
  html body #screen-editor #ethvelleClipFaceActionsBackdrop .evpa-choice.evpa-choice-add .evpa-choice-preview .evpa-choice-add-icon{
    border:0!important;outline:0!important;box-shadow:none!important;
    -webkit-box-shadow:none!important;
  }
</style>
<script id="ethvelle-v148-held-items-both-hands-layout-fix">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  if(window.__ethvelleV148HeldItemsInstalled)return;
  window.__ethvelleV148HeldItemsInstalled=true;

  const BACKDROP_ID='ethvelleCharacterPropsBackdrop';
  const deliberatelyCleared=typeof WeakSet==='function'?new WeakSet():null;
  let selectedSide='R';
  let pendingMirror=null;
  let observe=null;
  let arrangeRaf=0;

  function activeChar(){
    try{return typeof activeCharacter==='function'?activeCharacter():null;}catch(_e){return null;}
  }
  function getProps(char){
    if(!char)return null;
    if(!char.handProps||typeof char.handProps!=='object')char.handProps={L:null,R:null};
    if(!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(char.handProps,'L'))char.handProps.L=null;
    if(!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(char.handProps,'R'))char.handProps.R=null;
    return char.handProps;
  }
  function copyItem(item){
    if(!item||typeof item!=='object')return null;
    return Object.assign({},item);
  }
  function isCleared(char){return !!(deliberatelyCleared&&deliberatelyCleared.has(char));}
  /* Hand records are independent. Never populate the opposite hand automatically;
     the user must deliberately upload an object while that hand is selected. */
  function mirrorIfMissing(char){ return false; }
  function finalizePendingMirror(char){ pendingMirror=null; return false; }
  function updateSideButtons(side){
    const back=document.getElementById(BACKDROP_ID);
    if(!back)return;
    const row=back.querySelector('.evpa-action-side');
    if(!row)return;
    row.querySelectorAll('button').forEach(button=>{
      const label=String(button.textContent||'').toLowerCase();
      const isLeft=/left/.test(label),isRight=/right/.test(label);
      const active=side==='L'?isLeft:isRight;
      button.classList.toggle('active',active);
      button.setAttribute('aria-pressed',active?'true':'false');
    });
  }
  function arrangePanel(){
    const back=document.getElementById(BACKDROP_ID);
    if(!back||!back.classList.contains('active'))return;
    const sheet=back.querySelector('.evpa-props-sheet');
    if(!sheet)return;
    const handle=Array.from(sheet.children).find(el=>el.classList&&el.classList.contains('sheet-handle'))||null;
    const upload=sheet.querySelector('.evpa-prop-upload');
    const file=sheet.querySelector('#evpaPropFile');
    const preview=sheet.querySelector('.evpa-prop-preview');
    const sides=sheet.querySelector('.evpa-action-side');
    /* Desired order: Add Object -> object tile -> Left/Right Hand selectors.
       Move existing nodes; do not rebuild the panel or replace the live canvas. */
    let cursor=handle;
    [upload,file,preview,sides].forEach(node=>{
      if(!node||node.parentNode!==sheet)return;
      const ref=cursor?cursor.nextSibling:sheet.firstChild;
      if(ref!==node)sheet.insertBefore(node,ref||null);
      cursor=node;
    });
    selectedSide=selectedSide==='L'?'L':'R';
    updateSideButtons(selectedSide);
  }
  function scheduleArrange(){
    if(arrangeRaf)return;
    arrangeRaf=requestAnimationFrame(()=>{arrangeRaf=0;arrangePanel();});
  }
  function watchBackdrop(){
    const back=document.getElementById(BACKDROP_ID);
    if(!back||typeof MutationObserver!=='function')return;
    if(!observe){
      observe=new MutationObserver(scheduleArrange);
      observe.observe(back,{childList:true,subtree:true});
    }
  }

  /* Mirror a legacy one-sided object before opening so Right Hand is populated
     immediately when that side is the default. */
  const oldOpen=window.openCharacterPropsPanel;
  if(typeof oldOpen==='function'&&!oldOpen.__ethvelleV148){
    const wrappedOpen=function(){
      const char=activeChar();
      if(char)mirrorIfMissing(char);
      selectedSide='R';
      const result=oldOpen.apply(this,arguments);
      watchBackdrop();
      scheduleArrange();
      updateSideButtons('R');
      return result;
    };
    wrappedOpen.__ethvelleV148=true;
    window.openCharacterPropsPanel=wrappedOpen;
  }

  /* Before switching hands, copy the available object if the destination is empty.
     The v147 handler updates the UI in place, so no panel/canvas rebuild is introduced. */
  const oldSetSide=window.setCharacterPropSide;
  if(typeof oldSetSide==='function'&&!oldSetSide.__ethvelleV148){
    const wrappedSetSide=function(side){
      if(side!=='L'&&side!=='R')return;
      const char=activeChar();
      if(char)mirrorIfMissing(char);
      selectedSide=side;
      const result=oldSetSide.apply(this,arguments);
      updateSideButtons(side);
      scheduleArrange();
      return result;
    };
    wrappedSetSide.__ethvelleV148=true;
    window.setCharacterPropSide=wrappedSetSide;
  }

  /* The existing uploader always writes the new file to Right Hand. Remember the
     previous record identity; the drawing/persistence hooks mirror only after the
     uploader has actually replaced it, including when the same file is uploaded again. */
  const oldUpload=window.setCharacterHeldPropFile;
  if(typeof oldUpload==='function'&&!oldUpload.__ethvelleV148){
    const wrappedUpload=function(input){
      const char=activeChar(),props=getProps(char);
      if(char&&props){
        if(deliberatelyCleared)deliberatelyCleared.delete(char);
        pendingMirror={char:char,oldRight:props.R||null};
      }
      selectedSide='R';
      const result=oldUpload.apply(this,arguments);
      return result;
    };
    wrappedUpload.__ethvelleV148=true;
    window.setCharacterHeldPropFile=wrappedUpload;
  }

  /* Keep both hand records populated with the same uploaded image and starting
     transform. Each side remains independently adjustable after that copy. */
  const oldDrawProps=window.ethvelleDrawCharacterProps;
  if(typeof oldDrawProps==='function'&&!oldDrawProps.__ethvelleV148){
    const wrappedDrawProps=function(ctx,char,boneStart,boneEnd){
      if(char){
        if(!finalizePendingMirror(char))mirrorIfMissing(char);
      }
      return oldDrawProps.apply(this,arguments);
    };
    wrappedDrawProps.__ethvelleV148=true;
    window.ethvelleDrawCharacterProps=wrappedDrawProps;
  }

  /* Fallback for builds where drawStick does not paint a frame before persisting. */
  const oldPersist=window.persistProjectState;
  if(typeof oldPersist==='function'&&!oldPersist.__ethvelleV148){
    const wrappedPersist=function(){
      const char=pendingMirror&&pendingMirror.char;
      if(char)finalizePendingMirror(char);
      return oldPersist.apply(this,arguments);
    };
    wrappedPersist.__ethvelleV148=true;
    window.persistProjectState=wrappedPersist;
  }

  /* Explicit Remove clears both mirrored copies. Ordinary hand switching never removes
     anything; this is the only action that intentionally clears the object from both. */
  const oldRemove=window.removeCharacterHeldProp;
  if(typeof oldRemove==='function'&&!oldRemove.__ethvelleV148){
    const wrappedRemove=function(){
      const char=activeChar(),props=getProps(char);
      if(char&&props){
        props.L=null;props.R=null;
        if(deliberatelyCleared)deliberatelyCleared.add(char);
        if(pendingMirror&&pendingMirror.char===char)pendingMirror=null;
      }
      return oldRemove.apply(this,arguments);
    };
    wrappedRemove.__ethvelleV148=true;
    window.removeCharacterHeldProp=wrappedRemove;
  }

  /* If the app was already open when this block runs, keep its visible controls ordered. */
  watchBackdrop();
  scheduleArrange();
})();
</script>


<!-- Ethvelle v149: category-specific custom parts + Held Items two-way flip controls. -->
<style id="ethvelle-v149-custom-variant-and-held-controls-css">
  /* The model editor no longer offers the generic, untyped "Add part" uploader.
     Saved category-specific variants are displayed in the read-only list below. */
  #sheetEd #edLayers > .ed-chip-custom,
  #sheetEd .ed-extra-add-card,
  #sheetEd .ed-extra-help{display:none!important;}

  /* Show saved custom variants in the Model Lab without another generic upload area. */
  #sheetEd .ev149-saved-variants{margin:10px 12px 12px;padding:10px 0 4px;border-top:1px solid var(--line);}
  #sheetEd .ev149-saved-variants[hidden]{display:none!important;}
  #sheetEd .ev149-saved-variants-title{font-size:11px;font-weight:800;letter-spacing:.045em;color:#E9EDFA;margin:0 0 9px;}
  #sheetEd .ev149-saved-variant-group{margin:0 0 10px;}

  #sheetEd .ev149-saved-variant img{width:100%;height:40px;object-fit:contain;border-radius:6px;background:rgba(0,0,0,.16);}
  #sheetEd .ev149-saved-variant b{max-width:100%;font-size:9px;line-height:1.15;color:#F1F3FB;white-space:normal;overflow-wrap:anywhere;}

  /* Held Items: solid blue upload control, two independent flip blocks, hand selector below. */
  #screen-editor .evpa-props-sheet{display:flex!important;flex-direction:column!important;}
  #screen-editor .evpa-props-sheet>.sheet-handle{order:0!important;flex:0 0 auto!important;}
  #screen-editor .evpa-props-sheet .evpa-prop-upload{order:1!important;flex:0 0 auto!important;
    background:#2F5CE8!important;border:1px solid #2F5CE8!important;color:#fff!important;
    box-shadow:none!important;
  }
  #screen-editor .evpa-props-sheet #evpaPropFile{order:2!important;}
  #screen-editor .evpa-props-sheet .evpa-prop-preview{order:3!important;flex:0 0 auto!important;}
  #screen-editor .evpa-props-sheet .evpa-prop-stage-wrap{order:4!important;flex:0 0 auto!important;}
  #screen-editor .evpa-props-sheet .evpa-prop-controls{order:5!important;flex:0 0 auto!important;}
  #screen-editor .evpa-props-sheet .evpa-v149-flip-row{order:6!important;flex:0 0 auto!important;}
  #screen-editor .evpa-props-sheet .evpa-action-side{order:7!important;flex:0 0 auto!important;}
  #screen-editor .evpa-props-sheet>.evpa-close-btn{order:8!important;flex:0 0 auto!important;}
  #screen-editor .evpa-props-sheet .evpa-prop-upload:active{background:#244BCB!important;border-color:#244BCB!important;}
  #screen-editor .evpa-props-sheet .evpa-prop-flip-field{display:none!important;}
  #screen-editor .evpa-props-sheet .evpa-v149-flip-row{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(2,minmax(0,1fr));gap:9px;margin:12px 0 10px;}
  #screen-editor .evpa-props-sheet .evpa-v149-flip-row button{
    min-width:0;min-height:42px;padding:8px 6px;border:1px solid #343746;border-radius:11px;
    background:#20212A;color:#D4D8E5;font-size:11px;font-weight:800;line-height:1.15;
  }
  #screen-editor .evpa-props-sheet .evpa-v149-flip-row button.active{
    border-color:#4F7CFF;background:#173CA9;color:#fff;box-shadow:inset 0 0 0 1px rgba(255,255,255,.08);
  }
  #screen-editor .evpa-props-sheet .evpa-action-side{margin-top:8px!important;}
  #screen-editor .evpa-props-sheet .evpa-action-side button{color:#F5F5F7!important;}

<script id="ethvelle-v149-category-specific-parts-and-held-flips">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  if(window.__ethvelleV149Installed)return;
  window.__ethvelleV149Installed=true;
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const safe=v=>String(v==null?'':v).replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
  let pendingPart=null, partInput=null, actionWireRaf=0, heldLayoutRaf=0, heldObserver=null;

  function activeChar(){try{return typeof activeCharacter==='function'?activeCharacter():null;}catch(_e){return null;}}
  function currentClip(){try{return typeof selectedClip!=='undefined'&&typeof getClipByRef==='function'?getClipByRef(selectedClip):null;}catch(_e){return null;}}
  function ensureVariants(char){
    if(!char||!char.sheet)return null;
    if(!char.sheet.customVariants||typeof char.sheet.customVariants!=='object')char.sheet.customVariants={headParts:[],expressions:[],gestures:[]};
    ['headParts','expressions','gestures'].forEach(k=>{if(!Array.isArray(char.sheet.customVariants[k]))char.sheet.customVariants[k]=[];});
    return char.sheet.customVariants;
  }
  function routeForSection(section){
    const heading=section&&section.querySelector('.evpa-section-title');
    const label=String(heading&&heading.textContent||'').trim().toLowerCase();
    if(label==='face expression'||label==='facial expression')return {category:'expressions',kind:'custom-expression',field:'customExpressionId',display:'Face expression'};
    if(label==='eyes'||label==='eye')return {category:'headParts',kind:'custom-eyes',field:'customEyeId',display:'Eye variant'};
    if(label==='mouth'||label==='mouth movement')return {category:'headParts',kind:'mouth-open',field:'customMouthOpenId',display:'Mouth artwork'};
    if(label==='left hand'||label==='left hand gesture')return {category:'gestures',kind:'left-hand-gesture',side:'L',field:'customGestureLId',display:'Left hand gesture'};
    if(label==='right hand'||label==='right hand gesture')return {category:'gestures',kind:'right-hand-gesture',side:'R',field:'customGestureRId',display:'Right hand gesture'};
    return null;
  }
  function ensurePartInput(){
    if(partInput&&partInput.isConnected)return partInput;
    partInput=document.createElement('input');partInput.type='file';partInput.accept='image/png,image/webp,image/jpeg,image/*';
    partInput.id='ethvelleV149SectionPartFile';partInput.hidden=true;
    partInput.addEventListener('change',handlePartFile);
    ( $('screen-editor')||document.body ).appendChild(partInput);
    return partInput;
  }
  function openSectionPicker(route){
    const char=activeChar();
    if(!char||!char.sheet){if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('This character needs a Model Lab sheet before adding custom parts',{icon:toastIcons.info,duration:2200});return;}
    pendingPart={route,charId:char.id};
    const input=ensurePartInput();input.value='';input.click();
  }
  function cleanPartName(name,route){
    const cleaned=String(name||'').replace(/\.[^.]+$/,'').replace(/[_-]+/g,' ').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim().slice(0,36);
    if(cleaned)return cleaned;
    return route.side==='L'?'Left hand gesture':route.side==='R'?'Right hand gesture':route.display;
  }
  function handlePartFile(){
    const file=partInput&&partInput.files&&partInput.files[0],pending=pendingPart;
    if(partInput)partInput.value='';pendingPart=null;
    if(!file||!pending||!pending.route)return;
    const char=activeChar();if(!char||char.id!==pending.charId||!char.sheet){if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('The selected character changed. Please try again.',{icon:toastIcons.info});return;}
    if(file.type&&!/^image\/(png|jpeg|webp|gif|avif)/i.test(file.type)){if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Choose a PNG, JPG or WebP image',{icon:toastIcons.info});return;}
    const reader=new FileReader();
    reader.onerror=()=>{if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Could not read this image',{icon:toastIcons.info});};
    reader.onload=()=>{
      const img=new Image();
      img.onerror=()=>{if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Could not open this image',{icon:toastIcons.info});};
      img.onload=()=>{
        try{
          const max=1024,ratio=Math.min(1,max/Math.max(img.naturalWidth,img.naturalHeight));
          const w=Math.max(1,Math.round(img.naturalWidth*ratio)),h=Math.max(1,Math.round(img.naturalHeight*ratio));
          const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');canvas.width=w;canvas.height=h;
          const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d',{willReadFrequently:true});if(!ctx)throw new Error('Canvas unavailable');
          ctx.clearRect(0,0,w,h);ctx.drawImage(img,0,0,w,h);
          if(typeof edVariantRemoveFlatBackground==='function')edVariantRemoveFlatBackground(canvas);
          const variants=ensureVariants(char);if(!variants)throw new Error('Model sheet unavailable');
          const route=pending.route,id='extra_'+Date.now().toString(36)+'_'+Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,7);
          const uploadedName=String(file.name||'Custom part').trim()||'Custom part';
          const item={id,name:uploadedName,fileName:uploadedName,originalFileName:uploadedName,kind:route.kind,src:canvas.toDataURL('image/png'),w,h,createdAt:Date.now()};
          if(route.side)item.side=route.side;
          variants[route.category].push(item);
          if(typeof updateModelAssetFromChar==='function')updateModelAssetFromChar(char);
          if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState();
          if(typeof window.closeClipCharacterActions==='function')window.closeClipCharacterActions();
          const idx=Array.isArray(characters)?characters.indexOf(char):-1;
          if(idx>=0&&typeof window.edOpenFromChar==='function'){
            const opened=window.edOpenFromChar(idx);
            if(opened&&typeof opened.catch==='function')opened.catch(err=>console.error('Could not open Model Lab after custom part upload',err));
          }else if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Custom part saved, but the Model Lab could not be opened',{icon:toastIcons.info,duration:2200});
        }catch(err){console.error('Ethvelle custom section upload failed',err);if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Could not save this custom part',{icon:toastIcons.info,duration:2200});}
      };
      img.src=String(reader.result||'');
    };
    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
  }

  /* Replace the old generic editor-navigation click handler with a section-aware file picker. */
  function wirePartAddButtons(){
    const back=$('ethvelleClipFaceActionsBackdrop');if(!back||!back.classList.contains('active'))return;
    back.querySelectorAll('.evpa-section').forEach(section=>{
      const route=routeForSection(section),grid=section.querySelector(':scope > .evpa-choice-grid');
      if(!route||!grid)return;
      const old=grid.querySelector(':scope > .evpa-choice-add');if(!old)return;
      if(old.dataset.evV149Route===route.field)return;
      const replacement=old.cloneNode(true);replacement.dataset.evV149Route=route.field;
      replacement.title='Upload '+route.display.toLowerCase();replacement.setAttribute('aria-label','Upload '+route.display.toLowerCase());
      replacement.addEventListener('click',e=>{e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();openSectionPicker(route);});
      old.replaceWith(replacement);
    });
  }

  function variantsFor(char,category){const v=char&&char.sheet&&char.sheet.customVariants&&char.sheet.customVariants[category];return Array.isArray(v)?v:[];}
  function currentActions(){const clip=currentClip();return clip&&clip.faceActions&&typeof clip.faceActions==='object'?clip.faceActions:{};}
  function insertCustomChoice(section,item,field){
    const grid=section&&section.querySelector(':scope > .evpa-choice-grid');if(!grid||!item||!item.id)return;
    const button=document.createElement('button');button.type='button';
    const active=String(currentActions()[field]||'')===String(item.id);
    button.className='evpa-choice evpa-custom-variant-choice'+(active?' active':'');
    button.setAttribute('aria-pressed',active?'true':'false');
    button.title=item.name||'Custom part';
    button.innerHTML='<span class="evpa-choice-preview"><img src="'+safe(item.src)+'" alt=""></span><span class="evpa-choice-label">'+safe(item.name||'Custom part')+'</span>';
    button.addEventListener('click',e=>{e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();if(typeof window.updateClipCustomVariant==='function')window.updateClipCustomVariant(field,item.id);});
    const add=grid.querySelector(':scope > .evpa-choice-add');if(add)grid.insertBefore(button,add);else grid.appendChild(button);
  }
  function renderCustomChoices(){
    const back=$('ethvelleClipFaceActionsBackdrop'),char=activeChar();if(!back||!back.classList.contains('active')||!char)return;
    back.querySelectorAll('.evpa-custom-variant-choice').forEach(el=>el.remove());
    back.querySelectorAll('.evpa-section').forEach(section=>{
      const route=routeForSection(section);if(!route)return;
      if(route.field==='customExpressionId')variantsFor(char,'expressions').filter(v=>v.kind==='custom-expression').forEach(v=>insertCustomChoice(section,v,'customExpressionId'));
      else if(route.field==='customEyeId')variantsFor(char,'headParts').filter(v=>v.kind==='custom-eyes').forEach(v=>insertCustomChoice(section,v,'customEyeId'));
      else if(route.field==='customMouthOpenId')variantsFor(char,'headParts').filter(v=>v.kind==='mouth-open').forEach(v=>insertCustomChoice(section,v,'customMouthOpenId'));
      else if(route.field==='customGestureLId')variantsFor(char,'gestures').filter(v=>v.side==='L'||(!v.side&&v.kind!=='right-hand-gesture')).forEach(v=>insertCustomChoice(section,v,'customGestureLId'));
      else if(route.field==='customGestureRId')variantsFor(char,'gestures').filter(v=>v.side==='R'||(!v.side&&v.kind!=='left-hand-gesture')).forEach(v=>insertCustomChoice(section,v,'customGestureRId'));
    });
  }
  function scheduleActionWiring(){if(actionWireRaf)return;actionWireRaf=requestAnimationFrame(()=>{actionWireRaf=0;wirePartAddButtons();renderCustomChoices();});}

  window.updateClipCustomVariant=function(field,id){
    const clip=currentClip(),char=activeChar();if(!clip||!char||!char.sheet)return;
    const allowed=['customExpressionId','customEyeId','customMouthOpenId','customMouthClosedId','customGestureLId','customGestureRId'];
    if(!allowed.includes(field))return;
    const a=Object.assign({},clip.faceActions||{});a[field]=String(id||'')||null;
    if(field==='customExpressionId'){a.expression='none';a.customEyeId=null;a.customMouthOpenId=null;a.customMouthClosedId=null;}
    else if(field==='customEyeId'){a.eyes='closed';a.expression='none';a.customExpressionId=null;}
    else if(field==='customMouthOpenId'){a.mouth='talk';a.customMouthClosedId=null;}
    else if(field==='customMouthClosedId'){a.mouth='closed';a.customMouthOpenId=null;}
    else if(field==='customGestureLId')a.leftHand='none';
    else if(field==='customGestureRId')a.rightHand='none';
    clip.faceActions=a;
    if(typeof pausePlayback==='function')pausePlayback();
    if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState();
    if(typeof renderFrame==='function')renderFrame();else if(typeof drawStick==='function')drawStick();
    if(typeof window.openClipCharacterActions==='function')window.openClipCharacterActions();
  };
